### `daily.html`（2.1 KB）
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-Hant" class="">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>TOEIC Daily Coach</title>
<script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
<script>tailwind.config={darkMode:'class'}</script>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="style.css">
<script src="cloud-sync.js"></script>
<script>
/* 字級：4 段（小／標準／大／特大），存在這台裝置（不跨裝置同步）。第一次使用時手機預設「大」 */
var FONT_LV=[14,16,18,20],FONT_NM=['小','標準','大','特大'];
function fontGet(){var v=parseInt(localStorage.getItem('dailyfont'));if(v>=0&&v<FONT_LV.length)return v;return window.matchMedia&&matchMedia('(max-width:767px)').matches?2:1}
function fontApply(){document.documentElement.style.fontSize=FONT_LV[fontGet()]+'px'}
function fontCycle(){var n=(fontGet()+1)%FONT_LV.length;try{localStorage.setItem('dailyfont',n)}catch(e){}fontApply();
  if(typeof dnToast==='function')dnToast('字級：'+FONT_NM[n]);
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-fontlbl]').forEach(function(e){e.textContent=FONT_NM[n]});
  if(typeof render==='function')try{render()}catch(e){}}
fontApply();
</script>
</head>
<body class="bg-slate-50 text-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:text-slate-200">
<div class="flex flex-col md:flex-row min-h-screen">
  <aside id="side" class="sticky top-0 z-30 max-h-screen md:max-h-none md:w-72 md:shrink-0 md:h-screen overflow-y-auto border-b md:border-b-0 md:border-r border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 p-3 md:p-4"></aside>
  <div class="flex flex-1 min-w-0">
    <main id="main" class="flex-1 min-w-0 p-3 pb-28 md:p-8 md:pb-8 max-w-4xl"></main>
  </div>
</div>
<div id="mini" class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-40 md:hidden"></div>

<script src="audio.js"></script>
<script src="timing.js"></script>
<script src="speak.js"></script>
<script src="notes.js"></script>
<script src="newwords.js"></script>
<script src="tsl.js"></script>
<script src="daily.js"></script>
<script src="pron.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

### `cloud-sync.js`（8.9 KB）
```js
/* cloud-sync.js — Google 登入 + Firestore 雲端同步 localStorage（鍵名以 toeic 開頭者）
   用法：每個要同步的頁面，在「會讀寫 localStorage 的 script」之前加上
         <script src="cloud-sync.js"></script>
   設定：把下面 FIREBASE_CONFIG 換成你的 Firebase 專案設定（見說明）。 */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  const FIREBASE_CONFIG = {
    apiKey: '***REDACTED***',
    authDomain: 'toeic-web-5d5c1.firebaseapp.com',
    projectId: 'toeic-web-5d5c1',
    appId: '1:174019544058:web:62e9cd6decc7a0fca65a43'
  };

  const SDK = 'https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/';
  const META = 'syncMetaV1';                       // 本機同步狀態（不會被同步）
  const isSync = k => typeof k === 'string' && /^toeic/i.test(k);
  const ls = window.localStorage;
  const rawGet = Storage.prototype.getItem, rawSet = Storage.prototype.setItem, rawRemove = Storage.prototype.removeItem;
  const fmtT = t => t ? new Date(t).toLocaleString('zh-TW', { hour12: false }) : '未知';
  const isEmpty = v => v === null || v === '' || v === '{}' || v === '[]';

  let meta = { base: {}, dirty: {}, mod: {}, last: 0, cloudLast: 0 };
  try { meta = Object.assign(meta, JSON.parse(rawGet.call(ls, META) || '{}')); } catch (e) {}
  const saveMeta = () => { try { rawSet.call(ls, META, JSON.stringify(meta)); } catch (e) {} };

  let applying = false, user = null, db = null, timer = null, busy = false, status = 'out';

  /* ---- 攔截 localStorage 寫入，標記為「待上傳」 ---- */
  function markDirty(k) { meta.dirty[k] = 1; meta.mod[k] = Date.now(); saveMeta(); schedule(); }
  Storage.prototype.setItem = function (k, v) { rawSet.call(this, k, v); if (this === ls && !applying && isSync(k)) markDirty(k); };
  Storage.prototype.removeItem = function (k) { rawRemove.call(this, k); if (this === ls && !applying && isSync(k)) markDirty(k); };

  function schedule() { if (!user) return; clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(() => sync(), 1500); }
  window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => { if (user && Object.keys(meta.dirty).length) sync(); });

  /* ---- 合併：兩邊都有變更時 ---- */
  function mergeById(a, b) {
    try {
      const A = JSON.parse(a), B = JSON.parse(b);
      if (!Array.isArray(A) || !Array.isArray(B) || ![...A, ...B].every(x => x && x.id)) return null;
      const m = new Map(); [...B, ...A].forEach(x => m.set(x.id, x));
      return JSON.stringify([...m.values()].sort((x, y) => (y.t || 0) - (x.t || 0)));
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }

  /* ---- 同步主流程 ---- */
  async function sync() {
    if (!user || busy) return;
    busy = true; setStatus('sync');
    try {
      const col = db.collection('users').doc(user.uid).collection('data');
      const snap = await col.get();
      const cloud = {}; snap.forEach(d => { cloud[d.id] = d.data(); });
      const keys = new Set(Object.keys(cloud));
      for (let i = 0; i < ls.length; i++) { const k = ls.key(i); if (isSync(k)) keys.add(k); }
      Object.keys(meta.dirty).forEach(k => keys.add(k));

      const pull = [], push = [];
      keys.forEach(key => {
        const c = cloud[key], cv = c && c.value != null ? c.value : null;
        const local = rawGet.call(ls, key), dirty = !!meta.dirty[key];
        if (cv === null) { if (dirty || !isEmpty(local)) push.push(key); return; }
        if (local === cv) { meta.base[key] = c.ts; delete meta.dirty[key]; return; }
        if (isEmpty(local) && !dirty) { pull.push([key, cv, c.ts]); return; }
        if (c.ts === meta.base[key]) { push.push(key); return; }          // 雲端沒變，本機有改
        if (!dirty && meta.base[key] !== undefined) { pull.push([key, cv, c.ts]); return; } // 雲端有改，本機沒改
        // 兩邊都有不同內容 → 衝突
        const merged = mergeById(local, cv);
        if (merged) { applying = true; rawSet.call(ls, key, merged); applying = false; push.push(key); pull.merged = true; }
        else if (confirm('「' + key + '」雲端與本機內容不同。\n\n雲端最後存檔：' + fmtT(c.ts) + '\n本機最後修改：' + fmtT(meta.mod[key]) + '\n\n按「確定」＝使用雲端版本（本機被覆蓋）\n按「取消」＝使用本機版本（雲端被覆蓋）')) pull.push([key, cv, c.ts]);
        else push.push(key);
      });

      const batch = db.batch(); const now = Date.now();
      push.forEach(key => {
        const v = rawGet.call(ls, key);
        batch.set(col.doc(key), { value: v, ts: now });
        meta.base[key] = now; delete meta.dirty[key];
      });
      if (push.length) await batch.commit();

      applying = true;
      pull.forEach(([k, v, ts]) => { rawSet.call(ls, k, v); meta.base[k] = ts; delete meta.dirty[k]; });
      applying = false;
      let latest = 0; keys.forEach(k => { const c = cloud[k]; if (c && c.ts > latest) latest = c.ts; });
      if (push.length) latest = now;
      meta.last = Date.now(); meta.cloudLast = latest || meta.cloudLast;
      saveMeta(); setStatus('ok');

      if ((pull.length || pull.merged) && Date.now() - Number(sessionStorage.getItem('syncReload') || 0) > 8000) {
        sessionStorage.setItem('syncReload', Date.now()); location.reload();   // 讓頁面用雲端資料重新載入
      }
    } catch (e) { console.error('[sync]', e); setStatus('err', e && e.message); }
    finally { applying = false; busy = false; }
  }

  /* ---- UI ---- */
  let btn, panel;
  function setStatus(s, msg) {
    status = s; if (!btn) return;
    btn.textContent = { out: '☁ 登入同步', sync: '☁ 同步中…', ok: '☁ ✔ 已同步', err: '☁ ⚠ 同步失敗', nocfg: '☁ 尚未設定' }[s];
    btn.title = msg || (user ? user.email + '\n上次同步：' + fmtT(meta.last) : '');
    if (panel) {
      panel.querySelector('#cs-who').textContent = user ? user.email : '';
      panel.querySelector('#cs-time').innerHTML = user ? '上次同步：' + fmtT(meta.last) + '<br>雲端最後存檔：' + fmtT(meta.cloudLast) : '';
    }
  }
  function buildUI() {
    btn = document.createElement('button'); btn.type = 'button';
    btn.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;right:12px;bottom:12px;z-index:99999;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:999px;border:0;background:#4f46e5;color:#fff;font:600 13px system-ui,sans-serif;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.3);cursor:pointer';
    panel = document.createElement('div');
    panel.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;right:12px;bottom:56px;z-index:99999;display:none;padding:12px;border-radius:12px;background:#fff;color:#1e293b;font:13px system-ui,sans-serif;box-shadow:0 2px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.35)';
    panel.innerHTML = '<div id="cs-who" style="margin-bottom:4px;word-break:break-all"></div>' +
      '<div id="cs-time" style="margin-bottom:8px;font-size:12px;color:#64748b;line-height:1.5"></div>' +
      '<button type="button" id="cs-now" style="margin-right:6px;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;background:#fff;color:#1e293b">立即同步</button>' +
      '<button type="button" id="cs-out" style="padding:6px 10px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;background:#fff;color:#be123c">登出</button>';
    document.body.append(panel, btn);
    btn.onclick = () => {
      if (status === 'nocfg') { alert('請先在 cloud-sync.js 填入 Firebase 設定。'); return; }
      if (!user) { signIn(); return; }
      panel.style.display = panel.style.display === 'none' ? 'block' : 'none';
    };
    panel.querySelector('#cs-now').onclick = () => { panel.style.display = 'none'; sync(); };
    panel.querySelector('#cs-out').onclick = () => { panel.style.display = 'none'; firebase.auth().signOut(); };
    setStatus(FIREBASE_CONFIG.apiKey ? 'out' : 'nocfg');
  }
  async function signIn() {
    const auth = firebase.auth(), p = new firebase.auth.GoogleAuthProvider();
    try { await auth.signInWithPopup(p); }
    catch (e) {
      if (e.code === 'auth/popup-blocked' || e.code === 'auth/operation-not-supported-in-this-environment') auth.signInWithRedirect(p);
      else if (e.code !== 'auth/popup-closed-by-user' && e.code !== 'auth/cancelled-popup-request') { alert('登入失敗：' + e.message); }
    }
  }

  /* ---- 載入 Firebase SDK ---- */
  const load = src => new Promise((res, rej) => { const s = document.createElement('script'); s.src = src; s.onload = res; s.onerror = rej; document.head.appendChild(s); });
  async function start() {
    buildUI();
    if (!FIREBASE_CONFIG.apiKey) return;
    try {
      await load(SDK + 'firebase-app-compat.js');
      await Promise.all([load(SDK + 'firebase-auth-compat.js'), load(SDK + 'firebase-firestore-compat.js')]);
      firebase.initializeApp(FIREBASE_CONFIG);
      db = firebase.firestore();
      firebase.auth().getRedirectResult().catch(() => {});
      firebase.auth().onAuthStateChanged(u => { user = u; if (u) sync(); else setStatus('out'); });
    } catch (e) { console.error(e); setStatus('err', '無法載入 Firebase'); }
  }
  if (document.body) start(); else document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', start);
})();
```

   規則：整題（Part 2 含問句＋全部回答句）到齊才用 mp3，否則整題用機器發音。
   哪些題目到齊由 audio/index.json（Scan總表與音檔.py 產生）決定，開頁時讀一次，播放時不再逐檔探測。讀不到清單（含雙擊開啟 file://）就全部用機器發音。 */

  setTimeout(done, 30000); return done;
}

function auApply(a) { a = a || AU.el; if (!a) return; try { a.defaultPlaybackRate = AU.rate; a.playbackRate = AU.rate; a.preservesPitch = true; a.webkitPreservesPitch = true; } catch (e) {} }
function auSetSpeed(v) {

'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800'}">${v}×</button>`).join('')}</span>`;

/* 組出播放清單：[lead(問句)] + 每個選項「字母 → 句子」；sentSrcs＝依抽到順序排好的句子網址，gapS＝句子後的停頓 */
function auSeq(sentSrcs, gapS, lead) {

}
const auFull = (part, id) => !!(AU.idx && AU.idx[part] && (AU.idx[part].complete || []).includes(id)) && !AU.bad[part + id];
/* 連續播放：全程重複使用同一個 Audio 元素（iOS 只要第一段從點擊啟動，後面換 src 都能繼續播） */
function auChain(srcs, gaps, live, done, fail) {

}
function auStop() { if (AU.el) { AU.el.onended = AU.el.onerror = null; try { AU.el.pause(); } catch (e) {} } }
function auCopy(t) {

  const fb = () => { const ta = document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value = t; ta.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;opacity:0'; document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); try { document.execCommand('copy'); } catch (e) {} ta.remove(); done(); };
  if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) navigator.clipboard.writeText(t).then(done, fb); else fb();
}
/* 維護頁的音檔區：逐句顯示檔名與有無，可複製檔名、句子，或一次複製「缺的」清單（檔名 | 句子）給 AI 語音工具 */
function auPanel(part, x) {
  if (x._legacy) return '';
  const n = auNames(part, x), rows = (n.q ? [{ name: n.q, text: x.q.t }] : []).concat(x._pool.map((p, i) => ({ name: n.s[i], text: p.t })));
  const I = (AU.idx && AU.idx[part]) || {}, full = (I.complete || []).includes(x.id), ms = (I.partial || {})[x.id], has = r => full || !!(ms && !ms.includes(r.name));
  const got = rows.filter(has).length, miss = rows.filter(r => !has(r));
  const lt = AU.idx && Array.isArray(AU.idx.letters) ? AU.idx.letters : [], ltLine = `<p class="text-[0.6875rem] text-slate-500 mb-2">🔤 選項字母 audio/A–E.mp3：${AU_LET.split('').map(c => `<span class="${lt.includes(c) ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-rose-500'}">${c}${lt.includes(c) ? '✔' : '✖'}</span>`).join(' ')}（本題需 ${AU_LET.slice(0, part === 'p1' ? 4 : 3)}；沒到齊就只播句子，不報字母）</p>`;
  return `<div class="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-3"><div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-2"><p class="text-xs font-bold">🔊 音檔 ${got}/${rows.length}${got === rows.length ? '（完整，播放用 mp3）' : '（未到齊，整題用機器發音）'}${AU.idx ? '' : ' · 尚未讀到 audio/index.json'}</p>${miss.length ? `<button onclick="auCopy(this.dataset.t)" data-t="${esc(miss.map(r => r.name + '.mp3 | ' + r.text).join('\n'))}" class="${btn} ${line} !py-1 text-xs">複製缺的（檔名 | 句子）</button>` : ''}</div>${ltLine}`
    + rows.map(r => `<div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs py-0.5"><span class="${has(r) ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-rose-500'}">${has(r) ? ### `timing.js`（3.9 KB）
```js
/* timing.js — Daily 的 timing 檢查（daily.html、speak.js、index.html 共用）
   切句規則與 daily_timestamps.py 的 split_sentences() 完全一致：
   腳本怎麼切、timing 就該長怎樣；文稿被改過或 timing 是舊的，這裡就會判成「異常」。 */
const TM_LABEL = { ok: '完成', partial: '部分', bad: '異常', none: '未做' };
const TM_COLOR = {
  ok: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-300',
  partial: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-950 dark:text-amber-300',
  bad: 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 dark:bg-rose-950 dark:text-rose-300',
  none: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400'
};
const TM_ABBR = new Set(['mr', 'ms', 'mrs', 'dr', 'inc', 'co', 'ltd', 'no', 'vs', 'st', 'jr', 'sr']);

/* 回傳 [[from, to], ...]：passage 中每個句子的字元範圍（與腳本相同） */
function splitSentences(text) {
  const res = [];
  let pos = 0;
  const add = (base, a, b, line) => {
    let seg = line.slice(a, b);
    const lead = seg.length - seg.replace(/^\s+/, '').length;
    seg = seg.trim();
    if (seg) res.push([base + a + lead, base + a + lead + seg.length]);
  };
  String(text).split('\n').forEach(line => {
    let st = 0, m;
    const re = /(?<=[.!?])\s+(?=[A-Z"'(])/g;
    while ((m = re.exec(line))) {
      const words = line.slice(st, m.index).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean), last = words.length ? words[words.length - 1] : '';
      if (TM_ABBR.has(last.replace(/\.+$/, '').toLowerCase()) || /^(?:[A-Za-z]\.){2,}$/.test(last)) continue; // A.M. / Ms. 等縮寫不切句
      add(pos, st, m.index, line);
      st = m.index + m[0].length;
    }
    add(pos, st, line.length, line);
    pos += line.length + 1;
  });
  return res;
}

/* state：none＝沒有 timing；bad＝欄位／範圍有誤，或與目前文稿的切句不符；
          partial＝有些句子沒對到（腳本顯示「✗ 未對到」的那些）；ok＝每個句子都有時間 */
function tmInfo(x) {
  const p = String((x && x.passage) || ''), tm = x && x.timing, exp = splitSentences(p);
  const res = (state, o) => Object.assign({ state, n: Array.isArray(tm) ? tm.length : 0, issues: [], gaps: [], dur: 0, total: exp.length }, o);
  if (!Array.isArray(tm) || !tm.length) return res('none');
  const issues = [];
  tm.forEach((t, i) => {
    const q = `第 ${i + 1} 段`;
    if (!t || ![t.start, t.end, t.from, t.to].every(Number.isFinite)) { issues.push(q + '：start／end／from／to 缺漏或不是數字'); return; }
    if (!(t.from >= 0 && t.from < t.to && t.to <= p.length)) issues.push(q + '：文字範圍（from／to）超出文稿');
    if (!(t.end > t.start)) issues.push(q + '：結束時間沒有大於開始時間');
    const o = tm[i - 1];
    if (i && o && Number.isFinite(o.to)) {
      if (t.from < o.to) issues.push(q + '：文字範圍與前一段重疊');
      if (t.start < o.end - 0.01) issues.push(q + '：開始時間早於前一段的結束');
    }
  });
  if (!issues.length) {
    const set = new Set(exp.map(e => e[0] + ':' + e[1]));
    tm.forEach((t, i) => { if (!set.has(t.from + ':' + t.to)) issues.push(`第 ${i + 1} 段：範圍與目前文稿的切句不符（文稿改過，或這是舊的 timing）`); });
  }
  if (issues.length) return res('bad', { issues });
  const got = new Set(tm.map(t => t.from + ':' + t.to));
  const gaps = exp.filter(e => !got.has(e[0] + ':' + e[1])).map(e => p.slice(e[0], e[1]));
  return res(gaps.length ? 'partial' : 'ok', { gaps, dur: tm[tm.length - 1].end });
}
const tmBadge = x => { const s = tmInfo(x).state; return `<span class="inline-block text-[11px] font-semibold rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 ${TM_COLOR[s]}">⏱ ${TM_LABEL[s]}</span>`; };
function tmWhy(x) { // 一句話說明 timing 缺什麼（完成則回傳空字串）
  const t = tmInfo(x);
  return t.state === 'ok' ? '' : t.state === 'none' ? '未做' : t.state === 'bad' ? '異常' : `漏 ${t.gaps.length} 句`;
}
```

### `speak.js`（47.9 KB）
```

### `newwords.js`（36.1 KB）
```js
/* newwords.js — TOEIC Daily Coach 的「🆕 新單字」
   載入順序：audio.js → timing.js → speak.js → notes.js → newwords.js → tsl.js → daily.js
   依賴（執行時才用到）：daily.js／notes.js 的全域 S／save／cur／DATA／idOf／esc／card／btn／main／render／speak／spStop／skStopAll／auCopy／
   dnKey／dnId／dnArr／dnArts／dnArt／dnMarks／dnBanked／dnExtraOf／dnFindExtra／dnFindExtraAny／dnExtraSrc／dnCtxOf／dnPrompt／dnModal／dnClose／dnToast／dnNav／dnGoArt／dnOpenExtra／dnInp／dnLine／dnPri／dnSm／dnChip／tslMap／tslNz

   資料：localStorage 鍵 toeicNewWordsV1（鍵名以 toeic 開頭 → cloud-sync.js 會自動同步；是「每筆都有 id 的陣列」，兩台裝置都有新增時會依 id 自動合併，不會跳衝突視窗）
     { id:'n…', text, type:'word|phrase', note, created, updated, t, base:[建立當下已有的文章 id], noauto?, own? }
     own＝1：想讓這個字獨立產生詳解，不要當成「收在別的字的屈折變化／衍生字裡」。它維持「待採用」，等新文章採用後才入庫（掛在那篇新文章底下）
   寫回主檔（daily.json）的欄位：extra_vocab 每筆可加 nw_done:[{ id, result:'added|covered|unclear', fix? }]
     → 由「入庫彙整」產生的 AI 提示詞要求 AI 回傳；網頁看到 nw_done 含該 id，就把這個新單字標為「已更新、可移除」。

   新單字的狀態（nwStatus）
     pending     待採用：還沒被任何文章採用、也還不在庫 → 會列進「新增文章」提示詞的「我的新單字」（優先於 TSL 候選字）
     waitdetail  已入庫、等 AI 詳解：有備註，但詳解還沒寫回 → 備註會隨詳解提示詞交給 AI
     supplement  待補充詳解：已有詳解，但備註還沒被 AI 處理 → 在「入庫彙整」的「新單字補充」複製提示詞
     updated     已更新：AI 已處理備註（added／covered／unclear）→ 可移除
     removable   已被文章採用或已在庫，且沒有備註要處理 → 可移除 */

const NW_KEY = 'toeicNewWordsV1';
const NW_OWN_NOTE = '使用者要求：請當成獨立入庫項目寫完整詳解，lemma 用它自己，不要併進其他字的詳解';
const NW_POOL = 5; // 每篇新文章最多優先帶入幾個新單字
let NW = null, NW_TM = null, NW_REC = null;
const nwList = () => { if (NW) return NW; try { NW = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(NW_KEY) || '[]'); } catch (e) { NW = []; } if (!Array.isArray(NW)) NW = []; return NW; };
const nwSave = () => { try { localStorage.setItem(NW_KEY, JSON.stringify(nwList())); } catch (e) {} };
const nwTouch = n => { n.updated = Date.now(); n.t = n.updated; };
const nwHasNote = n => !!String(n.note || '').trim();

/* ---------- 舊「單字紀錄」（index.html 舊版，鍵 toeicVocabV1）→ 併入新單字 ----------
   id 固定為 'nv_' + 舊 id：每台裝置各搬一次也不會重複（雲端同步依 id 合併）。舊鍵不動，當備份。
   nwMigratedV1（不以 toeic 開頭 → 不同步）記這台裝置搬過哪些，搬過後你刪掉新單字也不會又被加回來。 */
function nwMigrate() {
  let old; try { old = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('toeicVocabV1') || '[]'); } catch (e) { return 0; }
  if (!Array.isArray(old) || !old.length) return 0;
  let done = []; try { done = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('nwMigratedV1') || '[]'); } catch (e) {}
  const doneSet = new Set(done), have = new Set(nwList().map(n => n.id)), base = DATA.map(idOf);
  let add = 0;
  old.slice().reverse().forEach(o => {
    if (!o || !o.id || doneSet.has(o.id)) return;
    doneSet.add(o.id);
    const text = nwNormText(o.word), note = String(o.note || '').trim(), id = 'nv_' + o.id;
    if (!text || have.has(id)) return;
    if (nwList().some(n => dnKey(n.text) === dnKey(text) && String(n.note || '').trim() === note)) return;
    const tm = Number(o.t) || Date.now();
    nwList().push({ id, text, type: /\s/.test(text) ? 'phrase' : 'word', note, created: tm, updated: tm, t: tm, base });
    have.add(id); add++;
  });
  try { localStorage.setItem('nwMigratedV1', JSON.stringify([...doneSet])); } catch (e) {}
  if (add) { nwSave(); if (typeof dnToast === 'function') dnToast('已把舊「單字紀錄」的 ' + add + ' 個字併入新單字'); }
  return add;
}

/* ---------- 比對工具 ---------- */
function nwTslHit(text) { // TSL 核心單字（含 -s／-ed／-ing 等變化形）→ { w, r } 或 null
  if (typeof TSL_WORDS === 'undefined' || typeof tslMap !== 'function') return null;
  if (!NW_TM) NW_TM = tslMap();
  return (NW_TM && NW_TM.get(tslNz(text))) || null;
}
const nwEsc = s => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
function nwTok(t) { // 單字 → 含常見屈折變化的樣式
  const b = nwEsc(t), a = [b + '(?:s|es|d|ed|ing|er|ers|ly)?'];
  if (/e$/.test(t)) a.push(nwEsc(t.slice(0, -1)) + '(?:ing|er|ers)');
  if (/[^aeiou]y$/.test(t)) a.push(nwEsc(t.slice(0, -1)) + '(?:ies|ied|ier|ily)');
  if (/[^aeiou][aeiou][^aeiouwxy]$/.test(t)) a.push(b + t.slice(-1) + '(?:ed|ing|er|ers)');
  return '(?:' + a.join('|') + ')';
}
function nwRe(text) {
  const w = String(text).toLowerCase().match(/[a-z0-9]+(?:['’-][a-z0-9]+)*/g) || [];
  if (!w.length) return null;
  try { return new RegExp('\\b' + w.map(nwTok).join('[\\s-]+') + '\\b', 'i'); } catch (e) { return null; }
}
function nwFindArt(n) { // 這個新單字被哪一篇文章採用：核心單字／詳解（一律算）、文稿裡出現（只算建立之後才有的文章）
  const k = dnKey(n.text), base = new Set(n.base || []);
  for (const a of dnArts()) {
    if ((a.vocab || []).some(v => dnKey(v.word) === k)) return { x: a, via: 'vocab' };
    if (dnFindExtra(a, n.text)) return { x: a, via: 'extra' };
  }
  const re = nwRe(n.text); if (!re) return null;
  for (const a of dnArts()) {
    if (base.has(idOf(a))) continue;
    const m = re.exec(String(a.passage || '')); if (m) return { x: a, via: 'passage', hit: m[0], from: m.index };
  }
  return null;
}
function nwCovered(text) { // 這個拼法已收在別的字詳解的屈折變化／衍生字裡
  const k = dnKey(text);
  for (const a of dnArts()) for (const e of dnExtraOf(a)) if (dnArr(e.forms).concat(dnArr(e.derived)).some(f => f && f.w && dnKey(f.w) === k)) return { a, e };
  return null;
}
function nwBanked(n) { // 已在庫：入庫清單／核心單字／詳解，或收在別的字的屈折變化
  const b = dnBanked(n.text); if (b) return b;
  const m = dnMarks().find(q => q.nw === n.id); if (m) return { where: 'mark', qid: m.qid };
  const c = n.own ? null : nwCovered(n.text); if (c) return { where: 'forms', qid: idOf(c.a), lemma: c.e.text };
  return null;
}
const nwDoneRec = n => { for (const a of dnArts()) for (const e of dnExtraOf(a)) { const o = dnArr(e.nw_done).find(q => q && q.id === n.id); if (o) return { a, e, o }; } return null; };
function nwEntry(n) { // 這個新單字對應的詳解：{ a: 詳解所在文章, e: extra_vocab 那一筆 } 或 null
  const d = nwDoneRec(n); if (d) return { a: d.a, e: d.e };
  const k = dnKey(n.text);
  for (const a of dnArts()) for (const e of dnExtraOf(a)) if (dnKey(e.text || e.word) === k || (e.lemma && dnKey(e.lemma) === k)) return { a, e };
  const m = dnMarks().find(q => q.nw === n.id);
  if (m) { const x = dnArt(m.qid), s = x && dnExtraSrc(x, m.text); if (s) return { a: s, e: dnFindExtra(s, m.text) }; }
  const c = n.own ? null : nwCovered(n.text); if (c) return c;
  return null;
}
function nwStatus(n) {
  const ent = nwEntry(n), art = nwFindArt(n), bk = nwBanked(n), rec = nwDoneRec(n), note = nwHasNote(n);
  const adopted = !!(art || bk || ent);
  let st;
  if (!adopted) st = 'pending';
  else if (note) st = ent ? (rec ? 'updated' : 'supplement') : 'waitdetail';
  else st = 'removable';
  return { st, art, bk, ent, rec, note, tsl: nwTslHit(n.text) };
}
const nwIsDone = n => !!nwDoneRec(n);
function nwNotesFor(m) { // 這筆入庫項目還沒被 AI 處理的新單字備註
  const ks = new Set([dnKey(m.text)].concat(m.keys || [])), ids = new Set(Array.isArray(m.nw) ? m.nw : (m.nw ? [m.nw] : []));
  return nwList().filter(n => nwHasNote(n) && (ids.has(n.id) || ks.has(dnKey(n.text))) && !nwIsDone(n));
}
function nwNoteTxt(m) { // 提示詞「要寫的項目」每一行後面接的備註
  let t = m.note ? `｜我的備註：${m.note}` : '';
  nwNotesFor(m).forEach(n => { t += `｜新單字備註[id=${n.id}]：${String(n.note).trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ')}`; });
  return t;
}
const nwRules = () => [
  '【新單字備註（很重要）】',
  '- 標有「新單字備註[id=…]」的項目，備註是使用者自己記下的疑問或想學的重點，可能是語音輸入，會有錯字、同音字或中英夾雜（例如把 affect 打成 effect）。請先結合該單字與文章，判斷使用者真正想問什麼；看不懂、或明顯與該單字無關，就不要硬猜。',
  '- 備註主要用來決定「詳解要補強什麼」（用法、文法、易混淆、搭配、記憶方式…），請放進適當的欄位，格式與程度照上面的規則；不要把備註原文貼進去。',
  '- 如果備註想知道的內容，原本的詳解（或你這次要寫的內容）已經涵蓋，就不要為了回應備註硬加新的、也不要重複；一樣算處理完成。',
  '- 每一筆含新單字備註的項目，都要多一個 "nw_done" 陣列，每一條備註各一筆：{ "id": "（備註的 id）", "result": "added｜covered｜unclear", "fix": "（選填）你判斷備註裡打錯的字，例如 effect→affect" }。added＝已補進詳解；covered＝原本就有，沒有新增；unclear＝看不懂或無關，沒有處理。若該筆原本已有 nw_done，既有的一律保留，再加上這次的。',
  ''
];

/* ---------- 自動入庫：非核心單字被文章採用（只出現在文稿裡）→ 自動加入入庫清單，之後用「入庫彙整」請 AI 補詳解 ---------- */
function nwAutoMark() {
  let add = 0; const now = Date.now();
  nwList().forEach(n => {
    if (n.noauto || dnMarks().some(m => m.nw === n.id) || dnBanked(n.text) || (!n.own && nwCovered(n.text))) return;
    const a = nwFindArt(n); if (!a || a.via !== 'passage') return;
    const text = a.hit, p = String(a.x.passage || '');
    if (dnMarks().some(m => dnKey(m.text) === dnKey(text))) return;
    dnMarks().push({ id: dnId('m'), qid: idOf(a.x), text, type: /\s/.test(text.trim()) ? 'phrase' : 'word', ctx: dnCtxOf(p, a.from, a.from + text.length, text), from: a.from, to: a.from + text.length, note: n.own ? NW_OWN_NOTE : '', nw: n.id, auto: 1, created: now, updated: now });
    add++;
  });
  if (add) { save(); dnToast(`新單字已被文章採用，自動入庫 ${add} 個`); }
  return add;
}
function nwOnMarkDel(markId) { // 使用者自己刪掉自動入庫的項目 → 不要再自動加回來
  const m = dnMarks().find(q => q.id === markId); if (!m || !m.nw) return;
  const n = nwList().find(q => q.id === m.nw); if (n) { n.noauto = 1; nwTouch(n); nwSave(); }
}

/* ---------- 補充區：已有詳解、但備註還沒被 AI 處理的新單字 ---------- */
function nwSupGroups() {
  const by = new Map();
  nwList().forEach(n => {
    const s = nwStatus(n); if (s.st !== 'supplement' || !s.ent) return;
    const e = s.ent.e, a = s.ent.a, qid = idOf(a), ek = dnKey(e.text || e.word);
    const g = by.get(qid) || { qid, a, ents: new Map() }; by.set(qid, g);
    const it = g.ents.get(ek) || { e, ns: [] }; g.ents.set(ek, it); it.ns.push(n);
  });
  return Array.from(by.values()).sort((p, q) => p.a.week - q.a.week || p.a.day - q.a.day);
}
function nwSupItems(g) {
  return Array.from(g.ents.values()).map(({ e, ns }) => {
    const text = String(e.text || e.word).trim(), type = e.type || (/\s/.test(text) ? 'phrase' : 'word'), p = String(g.a.passage || ''), i = p.toLowerCase().indexOf(text.toLowerCase());
    const m = { id: '~nw-' + dnKey(text), qid: g.qid, text, type, ctx: type === 'sentence' ? text : (i >= 0 ? dnCtxOf(p, i, i + text.length, text) : text), note: '', nw: ns.map(n => n.id), keys: ns.map(n => dnKey(n.text)).concat(e.lemma ? [dnKey(e.lemma)] : []) };
    return { m, fill: ['nwnote'] };
  });
}
function nwSupPrompt(qid, rng) { const g = nwSupGroups().find(q => q.qid === qid); if (!g) return ''; const its = nwSupItems(g); return dnPrompt(qid, new Set(), false, rng ? its.slice(rng[0], rng[1]) : its); }
function nwSupCopy(qid) {
  const g = nwSupGroups().find(q => q.qid === qid); if (!g) return;
  const its = nwSupItems(g), b = dnBatchPick('sup:' + qid, its.map(o => o.m.id).join(','), its.length), t = nwSupPrompt(qid, b.nb > 1 ? [b.lo, b.hi] : null); if (!t) return;
  auCopy(t); dnToast(b.nb > 1 ? dnBatchMsg(b, b.hi - b.lo) : '已複製新單字補充提示詞，貼給 AI');
}
function nwSupSec() { // 掛在「入庫彙整」頁與「新單字」頁
  const gs = nwSupGroups(); if (!gs.length) return '';
  const n = gs.reduce((s, g) => s + Array.from(g.ents.values()).reduce((t, it) => t + it.ns.length, 0), 0);
  return `<section class="${card} p-4 mb-4"><h3 class="font-bold text-sm">🆕 新單字補充 <span class="text-xs font-normal text-slate-400">${n}</span></h3>
    <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 leading-relaxed">這些字已經有詳解，但你記的備註還沒被 AI 處理。按「複製 AI 提示詞」會連同現有詳解與備註交給 AI：AI 會判斷備註有沒有打錯字、原詳解是否已經涵蓋，需要才補，處理完會回傳 nw_done；合併進 daily.json 後，新單字就會顯示「已更新」。</p><div class="mt-2">${dnCapCtl()}</div>`
    + gs.map(g => `<div class="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800"><div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2"><button onclick="dnGoArt('${g.qid}')" class="font-bold text-sm text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 underline cursor-pointer">${esc(dnArtLabel(g.qid))}</button><button onclick="nwSupCopy('${g.qid}')" class="${btn} ${dnPri} !py-1 !px-2.5 text-xs">複製 AI 提示詞（${g.ents.size}${dnCapTag(g.ents.size)}）</button></div>
      <div class="mt-2 space-y-1.5">${Array.from(g.ents.values()).map(it => `<div class="text-xs"><button onclick="dnOpenExtra('${g.qid}',this.dataset.t)" data-t="${esc(it.e.text || it.e.word)}" class="font-bold underline decoration-dotted cursor-pointer">${esc(it.e.text || it.e.word)}</button>${it.ns.map(x => `<p class="text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 break-words">備註：${esc(x.note)}</p>`).join('')}</div>`).join('')}</div></div>`).join('')
    + `</section>`;
}

/* ---------- 新增文章提示詞：我的新單字（優先）---------- */
function nwPending() { return nwList().map(n => ({ n, s: nwStatus(n) })).filter(o => o.s.st === 'pending').sort((a, b) => (a.n.created || 0) - (b.n.created || 0)).slice(0, NW_POOL); }
function nwPoolKeys() { return new Set(nwPending().map(o => tslNz(o.n.text))); } // 供 tslUnused 排除：已列為新單字的字，不要再當 TSL 候選字重複出現
function nwGenLines() {
  const ps = nwPending(); if (!ps.length) return [];
  const L = ['【我的新單字（優先採用）】', `・以下是使用者自己遇到、想學的新單字／片語（${ps.length} 個，依加入順序）。原則上請用 2–5 個自然地寫進本篇 passage（不是硬性規定，可依文章情境增加或減少），優先順序高於下方的 TSL 候選單字，目標是與 TSL 候選字大約各占一半：`];
  ps.forEach(({ n, s }, i) => L.push(`  ${i + 1}. ${n.text}（${n.type === 'phrase' ? '片語' : '單字'}｜${s.tsl ? 'TSL 核心單字 #' + s.tsl.r : '非 TSL 核心單字'}）` + (nwHasNote(n) ? `｜備註：${String(n.note).trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ')}` : '') + (n.own ? '｜獨立詳解：請當成獨立項目，lemma 用它自己，不併進其他字' : '')));
  L.push('・情境真的放不進本篇主題的字可以跳過，不要硬塞（沒用到的會留給之後的文章）；單字可用原形或自然的屈折變化，片語要照原樣出現在 passage 裡。',
    '・標「TSL 核心單字」的字：除了寫進 passage，也要放進 vocab（算在 vocab 總數 3–6 個之內，並優先於 TSL 候選單字）。',
    '・標「非 TSL 核心單字」的字：寫進 passage 即可，不要放進 vocab；文章合併進 daily.json 後，網頁會自動把它入庫，之後再用「入庫彙整」請 AI 補詳解。',
    '・備註主要是之後補詳解用的（使用者的疑問或想學的用法），是否反映在文章裡由你判斷：例如備註說想學某個用法，可以讓文章剛好用到；備註可能有錯字（語音輸入），請自行判斷原意，不要把備註原文抄進文章。', '');
  return L;
}
function nwGenPanel() {
  const ps = nwPending();
  return `<div class="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-3 mb-3 text-xs"><p class="font-bold mb-1">🆕 我的新單字（優先帶入提示詞）</p>${ps.length
    ? `<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1 mb-1">${ps.map(({ n, s }) => `<span class="inline-block rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 ${s.tsl ? 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800 dark:bg-indigo-950 dark:text-indigo-300' : 'bg-violet-100 text-violet-800 dark:bg-violet-950 dark:text-violet-300'}" title="${s.tsl ? 'TSL 核心單字：會放進 vocab' : '非核心單字：只寫進文章，之後自動入庫'}">${esc(n.text)}${nwHasNote(n) ? ' 📝' : ''}</span>`).join('')}</div><p class="text-slate-500">藍色＝TSL 核心單字（會放進 vocab）；紫色＝非核心單字（只寫進文章，合併後自動入庫）；📝＝有備註。最多帶 ${NW_POOL} 個，其餘留給之後的文章。</p>`
    : '<p class="text-slate-500">目前沒有待採用的新單字。</p>'}</div>`;
}

/* ---------- 語音輸入（Web Speech API）---------- */
/* 備註欄語音語言：中／EN 切換，記在這台裝置 */
function nwLang() { try { return localStorage.getItem('nwmiclang') === 'en-US' ? 'en-US' : 'zh-TW'; } catch (e) { return 'zh-TW'; } }
const nwLangLbl = () => nwLang() === 'en-US' ? 'EN' : '中';
function nwLangToggle() {
  const n = nwLang() === 'en-US' ? 'zh-TW' : 'en-US';
  try { localStorage.setItem('nwmiclang', n); } catch (e) {}
  try { if (typeof NW_REC !== 'undefined' && NW_REC) NW_REC.stop(); } catch (e) {}
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-nwlang]').forEach(b => { b.textContent = nwLangLbl(); });
  if (typeof dnToast === 'function') dnToast(n === 'en-US' ? '備註語音：English' : '備註語音：中文');
}
const nwNoteMic = (id, cls) => `<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5 shrink-0"><button onclick="nwMic('${id}',nwLang(),this)" class="${cls}" aria-label="語音輸入">🎤</button><button onclick="nwLangToggle()" data-nwlang class="${cls} !text-xs !font-semibold" aria-label="切換語音語言（中／EN）">${nwLangLbl()}</button></div>`;
const nwCleanWord = t => String(t || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[.,!?;:"“”()]+/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
function nwMic(id, lang, btnEl) { // 再按一次＝停止
  const SR = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
  if (!SR) { dnToast('這個瀏覽器不支援語音輸入，可改用手機鍵盤上的麥克風'); return; }
  if (NW_REC) { try { NW_REC.stop(); } catch (e) {} return; }
  const el = document.getElementById(id); if (!el) return;
  try { if (typeof spStop === 'function') spStop(); if (typeof skStopAll === 'function') skStopAll(); speechSynthesis.cancel(); } catch (e) {}
  const isW = el.tagName === 'INPUT', keep = isW ? '' : el.value, r = new SR(), alt = document.getElementById('nw-alt');
  r.lang = lang; r.interimResults = true; r.maxAlternatives = 5; r.continuous = false;
  const rest = () => { NW_REC = null; if (btnEl) { btnEl.textContent = '🎤'; btnEl.classList.remove('animate-pulse', 'text-rose-600'); } };
  r.onstart = () => { if (btnEl) { btnEl.textContent = '⏹'; btnEl.classList.add('animate-pulse', 'text-rose-600'); } if (alt && isW) alt.innerHTML = ''; };
  r.onresult = ev => {
    const res = ev.results[ev.results.length - 1], t = String(res[0].transcript || '');
    if (isW) {
      el.value = nwCleanWord(t);
      if (res.isFinal && alt && el.id === 'nw-w') { const seen = new Set([el.value]), opts = []; for (let i = 1; i < res.length; i++) { const c = nwCleanWord(res[i].transcript); if (c && !seen.has(c)) { seen.add(c); opts.push(c); } } alt.innerHTML = opts.length ? `<span class="text-xs text-slate-500 self-center">其他可能：</span>` + opts.map(c => `<button onclick="nwPickAlt(this.dataset.w)" data-w="${esc(c)}" class="${dnSm}">${esc(c)}</button>`).join('') : ''; }
    } else el.value = (keep ? keep.replace(/\s+$/, '') + ' ' : '') + t.trim();
    nwDraftSync(); nwHint();
  };
  r.onerror = ev => { const m = { 'not-allowed': '麥克風權限被拒絕，請到瀏覽器設定允許', 'service-not-allowed': '麥克風權限被拒絕，請到瀏覽器設定允許', 'no-speech': '沒聽到聲音，再按一次麥克風', 'audio-capture': '找不到麥克風', 'network': '語音辨識需要網路' }[ev.error]; if (m) dnToast(m); };
  r.onend = rest;
  NW_REC = r; try { r.start(); } catch (e) { rest(); }
}
function nwPickAlt(w) { const el = document.getElementById('nw-w'); if (!el) return; el.value = w; const a = document.getElementById('nw-alt'); if (a) a.innerHTML = ''; nwDraftSync(); nwHint(); }

/* ---------- 新增／編輯／移除 ---------- */
DN.nwDraft = DN.nwDraft || { w: '', n: '' }; DN.nwTab = DN.nwTab || 'all';
function nwDraftSync() { const w = document.getElementById('nw-w'), n = document.getElementById('nw-n'); if (w) DN.nwDraft.w = w.value; if (n) DN.nwDraft.n = n.value; }
const nwNormText = t => { t = String(t || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' '); return /^[A-Z][a-z'’-]+$/.test(t) ? t.toLowerCase() : t; };
function nwHint() {
  const el = document.getElementById('nw-w'), h = document.getElementById('nw-hint'); if (!el || !h) return;
  const t = nwNormText(el.value); if (!t || !/[A-Za-z]/.test(t)) { h.innerHTML = ''; return; }
  const row = (c, s) => `<span class="block ${c}">${s}</span>`, out = [], tsl = nwTslHit(t);
  if (nwList().some(n => dnKey(n.text) === dnKey(t))) out.push(row('text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400', '⚠ 這個字已經在新單字清單裡（有不同的備註才能再加一筆）'));
  const bk = dnBanked(t), cv = nwCovered(t);
  if (bk) out.push(row('text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400', `✔ 已是入庫單字：${bk.where === 'vocab' ? '文章核心單字' : bk.where === 'extra' ? '已有詳解' : '在入庫清單'}（${esc(dnArtLabel(bk.qid))}）。仍可加入，備註會用來請 AI 補充詳解。`));
  else if (cv) out.push(row('text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400', `✔ 已收在「${esc(cv.e.text)}」詳解的屈折變化／衍生字裡。仍可加入；想讓它獨立產生詳解，加入後按該列的「單獨入庫」（會等新文章採用後再入庫）。`));
  out.push(row('text-slate-500', tsl ? `多益核心單字（TSL #${tsl.r}）：新增文章時優先排進文章與核心單字。` : '不是 TSL 核心單字：文章採用後會自動入庫。'));
  h.innerHTML = out.join('');
}
function nwAdd() {
  nwDraftSync(); const t = nwNormText(DN.nwDraft.w), note = String(DN.nwDraft.n || '').trim();
  if (!t) { dnToast('請先輸入單字'); return; }
  if (!/[A-Za-z]/.test(t) || /[\u4e00-\u9fff]/.test(t)) { dnToast('請輸入英文單字或片語'); return; }
  if (t.length > 80) { dnToast('太長了，新單字請輸入單字或短片語（句子請在文稿上選取入庫）'); return; }
  const dup = nwList().filter(n => dnKey(n.text) === dnKey(t));
  if (dup.length && (!note || dup.some(n => String(n.note || '').trim() === note))) { dnToast('這個字已經在新單字清單裡'); return; }
  const now = Date.now();
  nwList().push({ id: dnId('n'), text: t, type: /\s/.test(t) ? 'phrase' : 'word', note, created: now, updated: now, t: now, base: DATA.map(idOf) });
  nwSave(); DN.nwDraft = { w: '', n: '' };
  const s = nwStatus(nwList()[nwList().length - 1]);
  dnToast(s.st === 'pending' ? '已加入新單字' : '已加入（這個字已在庫' + (nwHasNote(nwList()[nwList().length - 1]) ? '，備註會用來補充詳解）' : '）'));
  render();
}
function nwDel(id) {
  const n = nwList().find(q => q.id === id); if (!n) return;
  const st = nwStatus(n).st;
  if ((st === 'pending' || st === 'supplement' || st === 'waitdetail') && !confirm('這個新單字還沒完成（' + NW_ST[st].label + '），確定要移除嗎？')) return;
  NW = nwList().filter(q => q.id !== id); nwSave(); dnClose(); render();
}
function nwRemoveDone() {
  const done = nwList().filter(n => { const s = nwStatus(n).st; return s === 'removable' || s === 'updated'; });
  if (!done.length) { dnToast('沒有可移除的'); return; }
  if (!confirm(`移除 ${done.length} 個已完成的新單字？`)) return;
  const ids = new Set(done.map(n => n.id)); NW = nwList().filter(n => !ids.has(n.id)); nwSave(); render();
}
function nwEdit(id) {
  const n = nwList().find(q => q.id === id); if (!n) return; DN.nwEd = id;
  dnModal(`<div class="${card} w-full max-w-lg p-4 md:p-5"><div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3"><h2 class="font-bold">🆕 編輯新單字</h2><button onclick="dnClose()" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 text-xl leading-none cursor-pointer" aria-label="關閉">×</button></div>
    <label class="block text-xs text-slate-500 mb-1">單字／片語</label><div class="flex gap-2 mb-3"><input id="nw-ew" value="${esc(n.text)}" autocapitalize="none" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" class="${dnInp} flex-1"><button onclick="nwMic('nw-ew','en-US',this)" class="${btn} ${dnLine} !px-3" aria-label="語音輸入">🎤</button></div>
    <label class="block text-xs text-slate-500 mb-1">備註（給 AI 補詳解用；改了備註會重新列入「待補充」）</label><div class="flex gap-2 items-start mb-3"><textarea id="nw-en" rows="3" class="${dnInp} flex-1">${esc(n.note || '')}</textarea>${nwNoteMic('nw-en', btn + ' ' + dnLine + ' !px-3')}</div>
    <div class="flex justify-between"><button onclick="nwDel('${n.id}')" class="${btn} text-rose-500">移除</button><button onclick="nwEditSave()" class="${btn} ${dnPri}">儲存</button></div></div>`);
}
function nwEditSave() {
  const n = nwList().find(q => q.id === DN.nwEd); if (!n) return dnClose();
  const t = nwNormText((document.getElementById('nw-ew') || {}).value), note = String((document.getElementById('nw-en') || {}).value || '').trim();
  if (!t || !/[A-Za-z]/.test(t) || /[\u4e00-\u9fff]/.test(t)) { dnToast('請輸入英文單字或片語'); return; }
  if (note !== String(n.note || '').trim()) { const old = n.id; n.id = dnId('n'); dnMarks().forEach(m => { if (m.nw === old) m.nw = n.id; }); save(); } // 備註改了 → 換新 id，舊的「已更新」紀錄就不再算數
  n.text = t; n.type = /\s/.test(t) ? 'phrase' : 'word'; n.note = note; nwTouch(n); nwSave(); dnClose(); render();
}
function nwTab(t) { DN.nwTab = t; nwRefresh(); }

/* ---------- 畫面 ---------- */
const NW_ST = {
  pending: { label: '待採用', c: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-950 dark:text-amber-300' },
  waitdetail: { label: '等 AI 詳解', c: 'bg-sky-100 text-sky-800 dark:bg-sky-950 dark:text-sky-300' },
  supplement: { label: '待補充詳解', c: 'bg-sky-100 text-sky-800 dark:bg-sky-950 dark:text-sky-300' },
  updated: { label: '已更新・可移除', c: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-300' },

(s.st === 'pending' ? `<button onclick="nwOwn('${n.id}')" class="${dnSm}">取消獨立</button>` : '') : (s.bk && s.bk.where === 'forms' ? `<button onclick="nwOwn('${n.id}')" class="${dnSm}">單獨入庫</button>` : '')}<button onclick="nwEdit('${n.id}')" class="${dnSm}">✎</button><button onclick="nwDel('${n.id}')" class="text-xs ${done ? ### `pron.js`（17.3 KB）
```js
/* pron.js — 發音練習：詳解／核心單字／生詞本／句型 裡每一個 🔊 旁邊都多一個 🎙，隨時可以練習、評分、記錄最高分
   依賴（執行時才用到）：daily.js 的 S／save／esc／find／cur／speak／spStop；notes.js 的 dnToast／dnAu；
                         speak.js 的 skCompare／skSR／skStopAll／skWordsH／skDiffH／skPctCls／skMsg／skCtl／skSeg／skLoose
   載入順序：放在 daily.js 之後（daily.html 已加）。
   做法：不改動既有每個 🔊 的產生程式，而是在畫面更新後，替每個 vplay(...)／speakPhrase(...) 按鈕補上 🎙；
         按下去開底部視窗：聽範例 → 錄音 → 辨識 → 逐字比對 → 分數。
   回放：錄音時同步用 MediaRecorder 錄下聲音，評分後可按「聽我的錄音」回放；錄音只存在記憶體（blob URL），
         關閉視窗／換字／重錄／切到背景／離開頁面時立即刪除，不會寫入 IndexedDB 或 localStorage。
   紀錄：S.pron[文字] = { b：最高分, n：練習次數, l：最近一次, t：時間 }（文字轉小寫、空白合併；同一個字不論在哪一篇都共用同一筆） */

const PR = { text: '', key: '', src: null, st: 'idle', final: '', interim: '', alts: [], cands: null, err: '', sr: null, tk: 0, restart: 0, stopping: false, srEnd: null, timer: null, res: null, nb: false,
  mr: null, stream: null, chunks: [], mime: '', mrDone: null, url: null, au: null, pl: false }; // mr…pl：自己的錄音（只放記憶體，關閉視窗就刪）
const prNorm = t => String(t == null ? '' : t).trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
const prWc = t => (String(t).match(/\S*[A-Za-z0-9]\S*/g) || []).length;
const prBest = k => ((S.pron || {})[k] || {}).b || 0;

/* ---------- 紀錄 ---------- */
function prRecord(key, pct) { // 回傳：這次是不是新的最高分（第一次練習不算「破紀錄」）
  S.pron = S.pron || {};
  const o = S.pron[key] || { b: 0, n: 0 }, first = !o.n, nb = pct > o.b && !first;
  o.n++; o.l = pct; o.t = Date.now(); if (pct > o.b) o.b = pct;
  S.pron[key] = o; save();
  return nb;
}
const prBadge = (key, extra) => { // 小徽章：最高分；沒練習過就不顯示
  const b = prBest(key);
  return `<span class="pr-badge ml-0.5 align-middle text-[10px] font-bold rounded px-1 py-0.5 ${b ? skPctCls(b) : 'hidden'}" data-k="${esc(key)}" title="最高分${extra || ''}">${b ? b : ''}</span>`;
};
function prRefreshBadges() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.pr-badge').forEach(el => {
    const b = prBest(el.dataset.k);
    el.className = 'pr-badge ml-0.5 align-middle text-[10px] font-bold rounded px-1 py-0.5 ' + (b ? skPctCls(b) : 'hidden');
    el.textContent = b || '';
  });
}

/* ---------- 在每個 🔊 旁邊補上 🎙 ---------- */
function prTextOf(btn) {
  const oc = btn.getAttribute('onclick') || '';
  if (/^\s*vplay\(/.test(oc)) return btn.dataset.w || btn.dataset.t || '';
  const m = /^\s*speakPhrase\((\d+)\)/.exec(oc);
  if (m) { // 簡報句型：省略號不念（與 speakPhrase 相同）
    try { const x = find(cur.w, cur.d), p = x && x.presentation_phrases && x.presentation_phrases[+m[1]]; return p ? String(p.phrase).replace(/\.{2,}|…/g, ' ') : ''; } catch (e) { return ''; }
  }
  return '';
}
function prInject(root) {
  (root || document).querySelectorAll('button[onclick^="vplay("],button[onclick^="speakPhrase("]').forEach(b => {
    if (b.dataset.pr) return;
    b.dataset.pr = '1';
    const text = prTextOf(b).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (!/[A-Za-z0-9]/.test(text)) return;
    const k = prNorm(text), m = document.createElement('button');
    m.type = 'button'; m.className = b.className.replace(/hover:text-indigo-\d+/g, 'hover:text-rose-600') + ' pr-mic';
    m.textContent = '🎙'; m.title = '練習發音（評分並記錄最高分）'; m.setAttribute('aria-label', '練習發音');
    m.dataset.pt = text;
    m.addEventListener('click', ev => { ev.stopPropagation(); prOpen(text, b); });
    b.after(m);
    m.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', prBadge(k));
  });
}
(function () {
  let q = 0;
  const run = () => { q = 0; try { prInject(document.getElementById('main')); } catch (e) {} };
  const start = () => {
    const m = document.getElementById('main'); if (!m) return;
    new MutationObserver(() => { if (!q) q = requestAnimationFrame(run); }).observe(m, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    run();
  };
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', start); else start();
})();

/* ---------- 練習視窗 ---------- */
function prOpen(text, src) {

  try { spStop(); } catch (e) {} try { skStopAll(); } catch (e) {}
  Object.assign(PR, { text, key: prNorm(text), src: src || null, st: 'idle', final: '', interim: '', alts: [], cands: null, err: '', res: null, nb: false });
  let ov = document.getElementById('pron-ov');
  if (!ov) { ov = document.createElement('div'); ov.id = 'pron-ov'; document.body.appendChild(ov); }
  prRender();
}
function prClose() {
  prCancel();
  try { speechSynthesis.cancel(); } catch (e) {} try { if (typeof dnAu !== 'undefined' && dnAu) dnAu.pause(); } catch (e) {}
  const ov = document.getElementById('pron-ov'); if (ov) ov.remove();
  prRefreshBadges();
}

  if (PR.pl) { prMineStop(); prRender(); }
  try { speechSynthesis.cancel(); } catch (e) {}
  if (PR.src && PR.src.isConnected) PR.src.click(); else speak(PR.text);
}
function prRender() {
  const ov = document.getElementById('pron-ov'); if (!ov) return;
  const rec = PR.st === 'rec', proc = PR.st === 'proc', r = PR.res, rc = (S.pron || {})[PR.key] || {}, long = prWc(PR.text) > 3;
  const warn = [];
  if (!window.isSecureContext) warn.push('麥克風需要 https 或 localhost；直接雙擊（file://）開啟時無法錄音。');
  if (!skSR()) warn.push('此瀏覽器不支援語音辨識（建議用 Chrome／Edge／Safari）。');
  const micCls = rec ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white animate-pulse' : proc ? 'bg-slate-300 dark:bg-slate-700 text-slate-500' : 'bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white';
  let res = '';
  if (r) {
    if (r.none) res = '<p class="text-sm text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400">沒有辨識到聲音，靠近麥克風再試一次。</p>';
    else res = `<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-2"><span class="rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-sm font-bold ${skPctCls(r.pct)}">${r.pct}%</span><span class="text-sm">${skMsg(r.pct)}</span>${PR.nb ? '<span class="text-sm font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">🏆 新的最高分！</span>' : ''}</div>
      <p class="leading-relaxed">${skWordsH(r.words)}</p>${r.pct >= 100 ? '' : skDiffH({ heard: r.heard, diff: r.diff })}`; // 100 分不顯示「你說的」對照與紅字／括號說明，有錯才出現
    if (PR.url) res += `<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mt-3"><button onclick="prPlayMine()" class="${skCtl}">${PR.pl ? '⏹ 停止回放' : '▶ 聽我的錄音'}</button><span class="text-[0.6875rem] text-slate-400">錄音只暫存在這個視窗，關閉就刪除</span></div>`;
  }
  ov.innerHTML = `<div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end md:items-center justify-center bg-black/50" onclick="if(event.target===this)prClose()">
    <div class="w-full md:max-w-xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto rounded-t-2xl md:rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 p-5 shadow-xl" style="padding-bottom:max(1.25rem,env(safe-area-inset-bottom))">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-3"><h3 class="font-bold">🎙 發音練習</h3>
        <span class="text-xs text-slate-500">${rc.b ? `🏆 最高 <b class="rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 ${skPctCls(rc.b)}">${rc.b}%</b> · 練習 ${rc.n} 次` : '還沒練習過'}</span>
        <button onclick="prClose()" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 text-2xl leading-none px-1 cursor-pointer" aria-label="關閉">×</button></div>
      <p class="${long ? 'text-lg' : 'text-3xl'} font-bold leading-relaxed break-words mb-3">${esc(PR.text)}</p>
      ${warn.length ? `<div class="rounded-lg border border-amber-300 dark:border-amber-700 bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40 p-3 mb-3 text-xs text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300 space-y-1">${warn.map(w => `<p>⚠ ${esc(w)}</p>`).join('')}</div>` : ''}
      <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-4"><button onclick="prListen()" class="${skCtl}">🔊 聽範例</button>
        <span class="text-xs text-slate-500 ml-auto">評分</span><button onclick="prLevel('loose')" class="${skSeg(skLoose())} !py-1 text-xs">寬鬆</button><button onclick="prLevel('strict')" class="${skSeg(!skLoose())} !py-1 text-xs">嚴格</button></div>
      <div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
        <button onclick="prToggle()" ${proc ? 'disabled' : ''} class="h-16 w-16 rounded-full text-2xl shadow ${micCls}" aria-label="錄音">${rec ? '⏹' : '🎙'}</button>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-500">${rec ? (long ? '🔴 錄音中…念完再點一次停止' : '🔴 請念出來…') : proc ? '⏳ 辨識中…' : '點麥克風開始（或按空白鍵）'}</p>
        <p id="pr-heard" class="min-h-[1.25rem] text-xs text-slate-500 italic break-words max-w-full">${rec ? esc((PR.final + ' ' + PR.interim).trim()) : ''}</p>
        ${PR.err ? `<p class="text-xs text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400">${esc(PR.err)}</p>` : ''}</div>
      ${res ? `<div class="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-left">${res}</div>` : ''}
      <p class="mt-4 text-[0.6875rem] text-slate-400 text-center">快捷鍵：空白鍵＝錄音／停止，Enter＝聽範例，Esc＝關閉</p>
    </div></div>`;
}
function prLevel(v) { S.spkLevel = v; save(); if (PR.cands) prScore(false); prRender(); }

/* ---------- 錄音＋辨識 ---------- */
function prToggle() {
  if (PR.st === 'rec') { prStop(); return; }
  if (PR.st === 'proc') return;
  try { speechSynthesis.cancel(); } catch (e) {} try { if (typeof dnAu !== 'undefined' && dnAu) dnAu.pause(); } catch (e) {}
  const SRc = skSR();
  if (!SRc) { PR.err = '此瀏覽器不支援語音辨識。'; prRender(); return; }
  prCancel();
  Object.assign(PR, { err: '', final: '', interim: '', alts: [], cands: null, res: null, nb: false, restart: 0, stopping: false, st: 'rec' });
  const tk = ++PR.tk, r = new SRc(), few = prWc(PR.text) <= 3; PR.sr = r;
  r.lang = 'en-US'; r.continuous = true; r.interimResults = true; r.maxAlternatives = 5;
  r.onresult = e => {
    if (r !== PR.sr) return;
    let itm = '';
    for (let k = e.resultIndex; k < e.results.length; k++) {
      const q = e.results[k];
      if (q.isFinal) { PR.restart = 0; PR.final = (PR.final + ' ' + q[0].transcript).trim(); PR.alts.push(Array.from(q).map(a => a.transcript)); }
      else itm += ' ' + q[0].transcript;
    }
    PR.interim = itm.trim();
    const el = document.getElementById('pr-heard'); if (el) el.textContent = (PR.final + ' ' + PR.interim).trim() || '…';
    if (few && PR.alts.length) prStop(); // 單字／短詞：辨識到一句就自動結束
  };
  r.onerror = e => {
    if (r !== PR.sr || e.error === 'no-speech' || e.error === 'aborted') return;
    PR.err = ({ 'not-allowed': '麥克風權限被拒絕，請在瀏覽器網址列允許使用麥克風。', 'service-not-allowed': '此瀏覽器不允許語音辨識服務。', 'audio-capture': '找不到麥克風，或被其他程式佔用。', network: '語音辨識需要網路連線。' })[e.error] || ('語音辨識錯誤（' + e.error + '），請再試一次。');
    prStop();
  };
  r.onend = () => {
    if (r !== PR.sr) return;
    if (PR.stopping) { if (PR.srEnd) PR.srEnd(); return; }
    if (PR.st === 'rec') { if (PR.restart++ < 3) { try { r.start(); return; } catch (e) {} } prStop(); }
  };
  try { r.start(); } catch (e) { PR.err = '無法啟動語音辨識，請再試一次。'; PR.st = 'idle'; PR.sr = null; prRender(); return; }
  prMediaStart(tk);
  clearTimeout(PR.timer); PR.timer = setTimeout(() => { if (PR.st === 'rec' && tk === PR.tk) prStop(); }, few ? 15000 : 60000);
  prRender();
}
/* ---------- 同步錄下自己的聲音（只存記憶體） ---------- */
async function prMediaStart(tk) {
  PR.mr = null; PR.chunks = [];
  if (!(navigator.mediaDevices && navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia) || typeof MediaRecorder === 'undefined') return;
  let stream;
  try { stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true }); } catch (e) { return; }
  if (PR.st !== 'rec' || tk !== PR.tk) { stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop()); return; }
  PR.stream = stream;
  const mt = ['audio/webm', 'audio/mp4'].find(t => MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported && MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(t)) || '';
  try {
    const mr = new MediaRecorder(stream, mt ? { mimeType: mt } : undefined);
    PR.mr = mr; PR.mime = mr.mimeType || mt;
    mr.ondataavailable = e => { if (e.data && e.data.size) PR.chunks.push(e.data); };
    mr.onstop = () => {
      stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
      if (PR.stream === stream) PR.stream = null;
      if (tk === PR.tk && PR.chunks.length) {
        if (PR.url) { try { URL.revokeObjectURL(PR.url); } catch (e) {} }
        PR.url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob(PR.chunks, { type: PR.mime }));
      }
      PR.chunks = []; PR.mr = null;
      if (PR.mrDone) PR.mrDone();
      if (tk === PR.tk && PR.st === 'idle' && PR.res) prRender(); // 辨識先結束、錄音檔稍後才好 → 補上回放鈕
    };
    mr.start();
  } catch (e) { stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop()); PR.stream = null; PR.mr = null; }
}
function prMineStop() { const a = PR.au; PR.au = null; PR.pl = false; if (a) { a.onended = a.onerror = null; try { a.pause(); } catch (e) {} } }
function prMineDrop() { // 停止回放並刪掉錄音（revoke 後記憶體即釋放）
  prMineStop();
  if (PR.url) { try { URL.revokeObjectURL(PR.url); } catch (e) {} PR.url = null; }
}
function prPlayMine() {
  if (!PR.url) return;
  if (PR.pl) { prMineStop(); prRender(); return; }
  try { speechSynthesis.cancel(); } catch (e) {} try { if (typeof dnAu !== 'undefined' && dnAu) dnAu.pause(); } catch (e) {}
  const a = new Audio(PR.url); PR.au = a; PR.pl = true;
  const end = () => { if (PR.au === a) { PR.au = null; PR.pl = false; prRender(); } };
  a.onended = a.onerror = end; a.play().catch(end);
  prRender();
}
function prStop() {
  if (PR.st !== 'rec') return;
  PR.st = 'proc'; PR.stopping = true; clearTimeout(PR.timer);
  const tk = PR.tk;
  const w = new Promise(res => { PR.srEnd = res; try { PR.sr.stop(); } catch (e) { res(); } });
  const ws = [w];
  if (PR.mr && PR.mr.state !== 'inactive') ws.push(new Promise(res => { PR.mrDone = res; try { PR.mr.stop(); } catch (e) { res(); } }));
  Promise.race([Promise.all(ws), new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1500))]).then(() => { if (tk === PR.tk && PR.st === 'proc') prFinish(); });
  prRender();
}
function prCancel() {
  PR.tk++; clearTimeout(PR.timer); PR.stopping = true;
  const r = PR.sr; PR.sr = null;
  if (r) { r.onresult = r.onend = r.onerror = null; try { r.abort(); } catch (e) {} }
  PR.srEnd = null; PR.st = 'idle';
  const m = PR.mr; PR.mr = null; PR.mrDone = null; PR.chunks = [];
  if (m) { m.ondataavailable = null; m.onstop = null; try { if (m.state !== 'inactive') m.stop(); } catch (e) {} }
  if (PR.stream) { PR.stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop()); PR.stream = null; }
  prMineDrop(); // 換字、重錄、關閉視窗、切到背景：舊錄音一律刪掉
}
function prFinish() {
  const r = PR.sr; PR.sr = null; PR.srEnd = null; PR.st = 'idle';
  if (r) { r.onresult = r.onend = r.onerror = null; try { r.abort(); } catch (e) {} }
  // 候選：只有一段時，辨識引擎給的前幾名候選都試，取最高分（單字常聽成同音字，第二、三名才是對的）
  let c = PR.alts.length === 1 ? PR.alts[0].slice() : [PR.final];
  if (PR.interim) c.push((PR.final + ' ' + PR.interim).trim());
  PR.cands = c.map(s => String(s).trim()).filter(Boolean);
  prScore(true); prRender();
}
function prScore(record) {
  if (!PR.cands || !PR.cands.length) { PR.res = { none: true }; return; }
  let best = null;
  PR.cands.forEach(h => { const c = skCompare([{ t: PR.text }], h); if (!best || c.pct > best.c.pct) best = { h, c }; });
  PR.res = { heard: best.h, pct: best.c.pct, words: best.c.sents[0].words, diff: best.c.diff };
  if (record) { PR.nb = prRecord(PR.key, best.c.pct); prRefreshBadges(); if (PR.nb) { try { dnToast('🏆 新的最高分 ' + best.c.pct + '%'); } catch (e) {} } }
}

/* ---------- 快捷鍵（視窗開著時） ---------- */
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  if (!document.getElementById('pron-ov') || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
  const t = e.target; if (t && (t.isContentEditable || /^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(t.tagName))) return;
  if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); prClose(); }
  else if (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Spacebar') { e.preventDefault(); prToggle(); }
  else if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); prListen(); }
}, true);
document.addEventListener('keyup', e => { if (document.getElementById('pron-ov') && (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Spacebar')) e.preventDefault(); }, true);
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if (document.hidden && document.getElementById('pron-ov')) { prCancel(); prRender(); } });
window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => { prCancel(); });
```

### `style.css`（2.3 KB）
```css
body{font-family:"Noto Sans TC","PingFang TC","Microsoft JhengHei",system-ui,sans-serif}
button:focus-visible{outline:2px solid #4f46e5;outline-offset:2px}
.tsent{cursor:pointer;border-radius:.25rem;transition:background .2s,box-shadow .2s}
.tsent:hover{background:rgba(79,70,229,.08)}
.tsent.active{background:rgba(79,70,229,.18);box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(79,70,229,.18)}
.dark .tsent:hover{background:rgba(129,140,248,.15)}
.dark .tsent.active{background:rgba(129,140,248,.3);box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(129,140,248,.3)}
/* 核心單字標示：不加 padding／粗體，字寬不變，開關標示時行數不會變；顏色淺 */
mark.hl{background:rgba(251,191,36,.24);color:inherit;padding:0;border-radius:2px}
.dark mark.hl{background:rgba(251,191,36,.20)}
/* 入庫標註：只用底線（不影響排版）；單字／片語／句子三種顏色 */
.mk{cursor:pointer;text-decoration-line:underline;text-decoration-style:dotted;text-decoration-thickness:2px;text-underline-offset:3px}
.mk-word{text-decoration-color:#0ea5e9}
.mk-phrase{text-decoration-color:#8b5cf6}
.mk-sentence{text-decoration-color:#10b981;text-decoration-style:solid;text-decoration-thickness:1px}

/* 標註模式：文稿只做選取，不當成可點播放的句子 */
.annot{cursor:text}
.annot .tsent{cursor:text}
.annot .tsent:hover{background:transparent}
.dark .annot .tsent:hover{background:transparent}

/* 播放回饋：按下有縮放、播放中脈動 */
button{-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent}
button:not(:disabled):active{transform:scale(.94)}
@keyframes spkp{0%,100%{opacity:1}50%{opacity:.35}}
.spk-on{color:#4f46e5!important;animation:spkp 1s ease-in-out infinite}
.dark .spk-on{color:#a5b4fc!important}
/* 手機輸入框至少 16px，iOS 才不會在點入時自動放大頁面 */
@media (max-width:767px){input,textarea,select{font-size:max(16px,1rem)!important}}
/* 詳解頁：可選取文字入庫 */
#detail-text{-webkit-user-select:text;user-select:text}
/* 文稿草稿差異（維護頁「✏️ 文稿」） */
ins.df-add{background:rgba(16,185,129,.22);text-decoration:none;border-radius:2px}
del.df-del{background:rgba(244,63,94,.18);text-decoration:line-through;border-radius:2px}
.dark ins.df-add{background:rgba(52,211,153,.25)}
.dark del.df-del{background:rgba(251,113,133,.25)}
.df-box{white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word}
```

（骨架：檔案共 4207 行；列出模組說明、常數、class／def 與其說明第一行）
模組說明：
  TOEIC Coach ─ 題庫合併工具（daily / photo / part2 / part3 / part4 / part5 / part6 / part7 共用）
  
  用法：把這支程式放在 daily.json、part1.json、part2.json、part3.json、part4.json、part5.json、part6.json、part7.json 所在的資料夾，雙擊執行。

       啟動時會自動選「資料夾內等待合併的副檔最多」的那一個；也可用  python json_merge.py part2  直接指定。
