### `daily_timestamps.py`（24.1 KB）
```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
daily_timestamps.py — 為 TOEIC Daily Coach 的 mp3 產生「句子時間戳記」並寫入 daily.json

使用方式：雙擊執行 → 選擇 mp3（可複選，檔名如 w1d1.mp3）→ 選擇 daily.json
          也可由 json_merge.py 的「執行對時」帶參數開啟：daily_timestamps.py --json daily.json a.mp3 b.mp3
          （有帶參數就不再跳出選檔視窗，直接處理）
原理：用 faster-whisper 聽出每個字的時間，再與 daily.json 裡該天的 passage 對齊，
      只在對應那一天新增／更新 "timing" 欄位，其他資料一律不動。
第一次執行會自動安裝 faster-whisper 並下載語音模型（約 150MB，需連網）。

【判斷標準】固定使用下方 CFG（不需要每次選）。想調整才改 CFG 的數字；語音練習的「寬／嚴」在網頁裡切換，跟這裡無關。
"""
import sys, os, re, json, subprocess, difflib, traceback

MODEL = "base.en"   # 想更準可改 "small.en"（較慢、模型較大）

# 一句話要同時滿足這些條件才算「對到」並寫入 timing；沒過的句子會在報告顯示 ✗，維護總覽會顯示「部分」
#   min_ratio：這句的字有多少比例在辨識結果裡找得到（舊版是 0.3，太鬆）
#   fuzzy    ：辨識聽成不同字時，相似度達多少才算（None＝不算，0＝一律算）
#   edges    ："any"＝句首或句尾至少一個字要對到（否則 start／end 會偏）；"both"＝兩個都要；None＝不檢查
#   max_rate ：每秒最多幾個字，超過代表時間被壓縮（正常約 2.5–4）；None＝不檢查（超過 3 個字的句子才檢查，太短的句子不準）
#   fill_gaps：True＝對不到的句子，若前後句都對到了且中間空檔合理，就依字數「推估」時間補上（報告顯示 ≈）；False＝不補
CFG = dict(min_ratio=0.60, fuzzy=0.70, edges="any", max_rate=8.0, fill_gaps=True)
ABBR = {"mr", "ms", "mrs", "dr", "inc", "co", "ltd", "no", "vs", "st", "jr", "sr"}


def norm(w):
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]", "", w.lower())


DIG = "zero one two three four five six seven eight nine".split()
SPEAKER = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z][A-Za-z.\-]*:")   # （舊）單字標籤，保留相容
# 說話者標籤（行首；與網站 speak.js 的 SK_LAB 同規則）：Customer: / The man : / Woman 1: / Mr. Lee:
LABEL = re.compile(r"^[ \t]*[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9.'’\-]*(?:[ \t]+[A-Za-z0-9.'’\-]+){0,2}[ \t]*[:：](?=\s|$)", re.M)


def label_spans(text):
    return [(m.start(), m.end()) for m in LABEL.finditer(text)]


def expand_words(words):
    """whisper 把數字寫成 64182 / 6-4-1-8-2 時，展開成 six four one eight two（時間平均分配）。"""
    out = []
    for s, e, t in words:
        n = norm(t)
        if not n:
            continue
        if n.isdigit():
            step = (e - s) / len(n)
            out += [(s + k * step, s + (k + 1) * step, DIG[int(c)]) for k, c in enumerate(n)]
        else:
            out.append((s, e, n))
    return out


def split_sentences(text):
    """回傳 [(from, to)]，為 passage 中每個句子的字元範圍。"""
    res, pos = [], 0

    def add(base, a, b, line):
        seg = line[a:b]
        lead = len(seg) - len(seg.lstrip())
        seg = seg.strip()
        if seg:
            res.append((base + a + lead, base + a + lead + len(seg)))

    for line in text.split("\n"):
        seg_start = 0
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?<=[.!?])\s+(?=[A-Z"\'(])', line):
            words = line[seg_start:m.start()].split()
            last = words[-1] if words else ""
            if last.rstrip(".").lower() in ABBR or re.fullmatch(r"(?:[A-Za-z]\.){2,}", last):
                continue  # A.M. / P.M. / Ms. 等縮寫不切句
            add(pos, seg_start, m.start(), line)
            seg_start = m.end()
        add(pos, seg_start, len(line), line)
        pos += len(line) + 1
    return res


def align(passage, words, cfg):
    """words: [(start, end, text)]（whisper 逐字結果）→ (timing 清單, 報告, 統計)。cfg＝判斷標準（見上方 CFG）。"""
    ptoks = []
    for m in re.finditer(r"\S+", passage):
        n = norm(m.group())
        if not n:
            continue
        if n.isdigit():   # 64182 → six four one eight two（與 whisper 結果的 expand_words 對稱，數字才對得上）
            ptoks += [(m.start(), m.end(), DIG[int(c)]) for c in n]
        else:
            ptoks.append((m.start(), m.end(), n))
    # 說話者標籤（行首的 Customer: / The man : / Woman 1:）音檔不會唸，不列入比對（句子範圍 from/to 不變）
    _ls = label_spans(passage)
    ptoks = [t for t in ptoks if not any(a <= t[0] < b for a, b in _ls)]
    wtoks = expand_words(words)
    pw, ww = [t[2] for t in ptoks], [t[2] for t in wtoks]
    sm = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, pw, ww, autojunk=False)
    match = {}
    for tag, i1, i2, j1, j2 in sm.get_opcodes():
        if tag == "equal":
            for k in range(i2 - i1):
                match[i1 + k] = j1 + k
        elif tag == "replace" and i2 - i1 == j2 - j1 and cfg["fuzzy"] is not None:
            for k in range(i2 - i1):  # 聽成不一樣的字：依相似度決定算不算
                if difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, pw[i1 + k], ww[j1 + k]).ratio() >= cfg["fuzzy"]:
                    match[i1 + k] = j1 + k
    timing, report, sents = [], [], split_sentences(passage)
    res = []   # 每句：[a, b, 字數, label, timing 或 None, 失敗原因]
    for a, b in sents:
        idx = [i for i, t in enumerate(ptoks) if a <= t[0] < b]
        hit = [wtoks[match[i]] for i in idx if i in match]
        label = passage[a:b].replace("\n", " ")[:50]
        if not idx or not hit:
            res.append([a, b, max(len(idx), 1), label, None, "未對到（音檔可能沒唸這句）"])
            continue
        ratio = len(hit) / len(idx)
        start = round(min(h[0] for h in hit), 2)
        end = round(max(h[1] for h in hit), 2)
        end = max(end, start + 0.1)
        why = None
        if ratio < cfg["min_ratio"]:
            why = f"只對到 {ratio:.0%}（需 {cfg['min_ratio']:.0%}）"
        elif cfg["edges"] == "both" and not (idx[0] in match and idx[-1] in match):
            why = "句首或句尾的字沒對到"
        elif cfg["edges"] == "any" and idx[0] not in match and idx[-1] not in match:
            why = "句首與句尾都沒對到"
        elif cfg["max_rate"] and len(idx) > 3 and len(idx) / (end - start) > cfg["max_rate"]:
            why = f"語速 {len(idx) / (end - start):.1f} 字/秒太快，時間可能被壓縮"
        if why:
            res.append([a, b, len(idx), label, None, why])
            continue
        res.append([a, b, len(idx), label, {"start": start, "end": end, "from": a, "to": b}, f"對中 {ratio:3.0%}"])

    # 補洞：連續幾句對不到、但前後句都對到時，依字數把中間的空檔分配給它們（推估值）
    if cfg.get("fill_gaps"):
        i = 0
        while i < len(res):
            if res[i][4] is not None:
                i += 1
                continue
            j = i
            while j < len(res) and res[j][4] is None:
                j += 1
            if 0 < i and j < len(res):
                t0, t1 = res[i - 1][4]["end"], res[j][4]["start"]
                n = sum(r[2] for r in res[i:j])
                gap = t1 - t0
                if gap > 0 and 1.0 <= n / gap <= 6.0:   # 空檔長度要像「真的有人唸了這幾句」
                    cur = t0
                    for r in res[i:j]:
                        d = gap * r[2] / n
                        r[4] = {"start": round(cur, 2), "end": round(max(cur + d, cur + 0.1), 2), "from": r[0], "to": r[1]}
                        r[5] = "≈推估（前後句夾住）"
                        cur += d
            i = j

    for a, b, n, label, t, note in res:
        if t is None:
            report.append(f"  ✗ {note}：{label}")
        else:
            timing.append(t)
            mark = "≈" if note.startswith("≈") else "✓"
            report.append(f"  {mark} {t['start']:6.2f}s – {t['end']:6.2f}s  {note}  {label}")
    return timing, report, len(sents)


# ───────────────────────── 文稿比對：以音檔為準，找出文稿與錄音不一致的地方 ─────────────────────────
# 用途：文稿定稿、錄完音後，又把文稿的字句刪掉或改掉（例如把 "The man :" 刪了再錄），
#       這裡用 whisper 聽到的內容當「事實」，和 daily.json 的 passage 逐字比對，列出每一處差異，
#       由人一筆一筆決定要不要改文稿（diff_passage → 畫面勾選 → apply_hunks → 重新對時 align）。
ALIAS = {"mister": "mr", "missus": "mrs", "doctor": "dr", "okay": "ok", "ms": "ms", "miss": "ms"}
_NUMW = set(DIG) | set((
    "ten eleven twelve thirteen fourteen fifteen sixteen seventeen eighteen nineteen twenty thirty forty fifty sixty "
    "seventy eighty ninety hundred thousand million and oh point dollar dollars percent am pm first second third "
    "fourth fifth sixth seventh eighth ninth tenth").split())


def _canon(w):
    return ALIAS.get(w, w)


def _is_numberish(ws):
    return bool(ws) and all(w.isdigit() or w in _NUMW for w in ws)


def _expand_passage(passage):
    """passage → [(a, b, 字)]；數字拆成英文數字（64182 → six four one eight two），每個字都對應原本那個詞的範圍。"""
    ls = []
    for m in re.finditer(r"\S+", passage):
        n = norm(m.group())
        if not n:
            continue
        if n.isdigit():
            ls += [(m.start(), m.end(), DIG[int(c)]) for c in n]
        else:
            ls.append((m.start(), m.end(), _canon(n)))
    return ls


def _expand_asr(words):
    """whisper 逐字 → [(start, end, 字, 原字)]；數字展開，原字只放在第一個。"""
    out = []
    for s, e, t in words:
        raw = (t or "").strip()
        n = norm(raw)
        if not n:
            continue
        if n.isdigit():
            step = (e - s) / len(n)
            out += [(s + k * step, s + (k + 1) * step, DIG[int(c)], raw if k == 0 else "") for k, c in enumerate(n)]
        else:
            out.append((s, e, _canon(n), raw))
    return out


def _strip_edge(w):
    return re.sub(r"^[^A-Za-z0-9]+|[^A-Za-z0-9]+$", "", w)


def diff_passage(passage, words, fuzzy=0.70):
    """回傳 (hunks, 略過的數字寫法差異數)。每個 hunk：
       {id, kind: delete|insert|replace|label, a, b, old, new, t0, t1, conf: sure|maybe, note}
       a/b＝這處差異在 passage 的字元範圍（insert 的 a==b＝插入位置）。"""
    pt, wt = _expand_passage(passage), _expand_asr(words)
    pw, ww = [t[2] for t in pt], [t[2] for t in wt]
    sm = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, pw, ww, autojunk=False)
    lspans = label_spans(passage)
    raw_ops = []     # (i1, i2, j1, j2)
    skipped = 0
    for tag, i1, i2, j1, j2 in sm.get_opcodes():
        if tag == "equal":
            continue
        P, W = pw[i1:i2], ww[j1:j2]
        if tag == "replace":
            if "".join(P) == "".join(W):                      # check-in ↔ check in
                continue
            if _is_numberish(P) and _is_numberish(W):         # 25 ↔ twenty five：寫法不同，不是真的差異
                skipped += 1
                continue
            if len(P) == len(W):                              # 一對一：聽成相近的字不算差異，只留真的不同的連續段
                bad = [difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, a, b).ratio() < fuzzy for a, b in zip(P, W)]
                k = 0
                while k < len(P):
                    if not bad[k]:
                        k += 1
                        continue
                    m = k
                    while m < len(P) and bad[m]:
                        m += 1
                    raw_ops.append((i1 + k, i1 + m, j1 + k, j1 + m))
                    k = m
                continue
        if tag == "delete" and _is_numberish(P):
            skipped += 1
            continue
        raw_ops.append((i1, i2, j1, j2))

    hunks = []
    for n, (i1, i2, j1, j2) in enumerate(raw_ops, 1):
        # 音檔時間窗：前一個對到的字的結束 ～ 後一個對到的字的開始
        prev_end = wt[j1 - 1][1] if j1 > 0 else 0.0
        next_start = wt[j2][0] if j2 < len(wt) else (wt[-1][1] if wt else 0.0)
        h = {"id": n, "t0": round(prev_end, 2), "t1": round(max(next_start, prev_end), 2), "conf": "maybe", "note": ""}
        if i2 > i1:
            a, b = pt[i1][0], pt[i2 - 1][1]
            h["old"] = passage[a:b]
        else:
            # 純插入：放在前一個字之後（沒有前字就放在第一個字之前）
            a = b = pt[i1 - 1][1] if i1 > 0 else (pt[0][0] if pt else 0)
            h["old"] = ""
        h["a"], h["b"] = a, b
        raw_new = [t[3] for t in wt[j1:j2] if t[3]]
        h["new"] = " ".join(_strip_edge(w) for w in raw_new if _strip_edge(w))
        gap = h["t1"] - h["t0"]
        if i2 > i1 and j2 == j1:
            h["kind"] = "delete"
            in_label = any(la <= a and b <= lb + 2 for la, lb in lspans) or any(la <= a < lb for la, lb in lspans)
            if in_label:
                h["kind"] = "label"
            need = 0.15 + 0.12 * (i2 - i1)
            if passage[:a].rstrip(" \t").endswith("\n") or not passage[:a].strip():
                need += 0.8          # 行首的字（多半是說話者標籤）：前面本來就會有換人說話的停頓
            if gap <= need:
                h["conf"], h["note"] = "sure", "音檔這裡只有 %.1f 秒空檔，確實沒唸" % gap
            else:
                h["note"] = "音檔這裡有 %.1f 秒的聲音，可能是辨識漏聽，請聽一下再決定" % gap
        elif i2 == i1:
            h["kind"] = "insert"
            at_edge = j1 == 0 or j2 >= len(wt)
            h["conf"] = "maybe"
            h["note"] = "音檔開頭／結尾多出來的字，常是辨識亂加（如 Thank you）" if at_edge else "音檔有唸、文稿沒有"
        else:
            h["kind"] = "replace"
            h["note"] = "文稿與音檔用字不同（音檔 %.1f 秒）" % gap
        hunks.append(h)
    return hunks, skipped


def _cut_span(text, a, b):
    """刪掉 text[a:b]，順手清掉殘留的冒號、多餘空白，並把新的句首字母改大寫。回傳新字串。"""
    m = re.match(r"[ \t]*[:：\-–—]+(?=\s|$)", text[b:])
    if m:
        b += m.end()
    before, after = text[:a], text[b:]
    at_line = before == "" or before.rstrip(" \t").endswith("\n") or before.rstrip(" \t") == ""
    if at_line:
        before = before.rstrip(" \t")
        after = after.lstrip(" \t")
    elif before.endswith((" ", "\t")) and after.startswith((" ", "\t")):
        after = after.lstrip(" \t")
    elif not before.endswith((" ", "\t")) and after.startswith((" ", "\t")) and after.lstrip(" \t")[:1] in ".,!?;:":
        after = after.lstrip(" \t")
    if before.endswith((" ", "\t")) and after.startswith("\n"):
        before = before.rstrip(" \t")
    if before.endswith("\n") and after.startswith("\n"):     # 整行刪空了 → 不留空行
        after = after[1:]
    # 句首大寫
    pre = before.rstrip(" \t")
    if pre == "" or pre.endswith(("\n", ".", "!", "?", '"', "”", "“")):
        mm = re.match(r"^([\"“'(]*)([a-z])", after)
        if mm:
            after = after[:mm.start(2)] + after[mm.start(2)].upper() + after[mm.end(2):]
    return before + after


def apply_hunks(passage, hunks):
    """把勾選的 hunks 套用到 passage，回傳新文稿。由後往前套，字元位置才不會跑掉。"""
    text = passage
    for h in sorted(hunks, key=lambda x: (x["a"], x["b"]), reverse=True):
        a, b, kind = h["a"], h["b"], h["kind"]
        if kind in ("delete", "label"):
            text = _cut_span(text, a, b)
        elif kind == "insert":
            ins = h["new"]
            if not ins:
                continue
            nxt = text[a:a + 1]
            if a == 0 or (b == a and text[:a].strip() == ""):
                text = ins + " " + text[a:]
            else:
                text = text[:a] + " " + ins + text[a:]
        elif kind == "replace":
            old = text[a:b]
            lead = re.match(r"^[^A-Za-z0-9]*", old).group()
            trail = re.search(r"[^A-Za-z0-9]*$", old).group()
            body = h["new"]
            if body:
                if old[len(lead):len(lead) + 1].isupper() and body[:1].islower():
                    body = body[0].upper() + body[1:]
                text = text[:a] + lead + body + trail + text[b:]
            else:
                text = _cut_span(text, a, b)
    return text


def transcribe_cached(model_getter, mp3, cache_dir, prompt=None, log=print):
    """辨識結果存成快取（backup/asr_cache/<檔名>.json）：同一個 mp3 沒改過就不用再聽一次。
       model_getter＝呼叫後回傳 whisper 模型的函式（沒有快取時才會載入模型）。"""
    os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
    st = os.stat(mp3)
    key = {"model": MODEL, "size": st.st_size, "mtime": int(st.st_mtime)}
    cp = os.path.join(cache_dir, os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(mp3))[0] + ".json")
    try:
        with open(cp, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            c = json.load(f)
        if c.get("key") == key and isinstance(c.get("words"), list):
            log("使用快取的辨識結果：%s" % os.path.basename(cp))
            return [tuple(w) for w in c["words"]]
    except Exception:
        pass
    words = transcribe(model_getter(), mp3, prompt)
    with open(cp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"key": key, "words": words}, f, ensure_ascii=False)
    return words


def dump_json(data):
    text = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    # 讓每個 timing 物件維持單行，避免 JSON 檔變得很長
    return re.sub(
        r'\{\s*"start": ([\d.]+),\s*"end": ([\d.]+),\s*"from": (\d+),\s*"to": (\d+)\s*\}',
        r'{"start": \1, "end": \2, "from": \3, "to": \4}', text)


def ensure_faster_whisper():
    try:
        import faster_whisper  # noqa
    except ImportError:
        print("第一次使用：正在安裝 faster-whisper（需要網路，請稍候）…")
        subprocess.check_call([sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install", "faster-whisper"])


def load_model(name):
    """只載入一次模型，多個檔案共用。"""
    ensure_faster_whisper()
    from faster_whisper import WhisperModel
    print(f"載入模型 {name}（第一次會下載）…")
    return WhisperModel(name, device="cpu", compute_type="int8")


def transcribe(model, mp3, prompt=None):
    print(f"辨識中：{os.path.basename(mp3)}")
    segs, info = model.transcribe(mp3, language="en", word_timestamps=True,
                                  beam_size=5, condition_on_previous_text=False,
                                  initial_prompt=prompt or None)
    words = []
    for seg in segs:
        print(f"  [{seg.start:6.1f}s] {seg.text.strip()[:60]}")
        words += [(w.start, w.end, w.word) for w in (seg.words or [])]
    return words


def main():
    import tkinter as tk
    from tkinter import filedialog, messagebox, simpledialog
    root = tk.Tk(); root.withdraw(); root.attributes("-topmost", True)
    here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

    # 命令列參數：--json 路徑 + 一或多個 mp3（由 json_merge.py 帶入）；沒帶就照舊跳選檔視窗
    argv, jpath, mp3s = sys.argv[1:], None, []
    k = 0
    while k < len(argv):
        if argv[k] == "--json" and k + 1 < len(argv):
            jpath = argv[k + 1]; k += 2
        else:
            mp3s.append(argv[k]); k += 1
    mp3s = [p for p in mp3s if os.path.isfile(p)]
    if jpath and not os.path.isfile(jpath):
        print(f"找不到 {jpath}，改成手動選擇。"); jpath = None
    if mp3s and jpath:
        print(f"由參數帶入：{len(mp3s)} 個音檔 → {jpath}")
    else:
        mp3s = filedialog.askopenfilenames(title="1/2 選擇 mp3 音檔（可複選）", initialdir=here,
                                           filetypes=[("音檔", "*.mp3 *.wav *.m4a"), ("所有檔案", "*.*")])
        if not mp3s: return print("已取消。")
        jpath = filedialog.askopenfilename(title="2/2 選擇要寫入的 daily.json", initialdir=here,
                                           filetypes=[("JSON", "*.json"), ("所有檔案", "*.*")])
        if not jpath: return print("已取消。")

    with open(jpath, encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
        data = json.load(f)
    # daily.json 可以是陣列，或含 _spec / items 的物件（目前格式）
    items = data if isinstance(data, list) else (data.get("items") if isinstance(data, dict) else None)
    if not isinstance(items, list):
        return messagebox.showerror("格式錯誤", "daily.json 需為陣列，或含 items 陣列的物件。")

    print(f"共選了 {len(mp3s)} 個音檔｜模型：{MODEL}")
    model = load_model(MODEL)
    done, failed, partial = 0, [], []

    for mp3 in sorted(mp3s):
        name = os.path.basename(mp3)
        print(f"\n===== {name} =====")

        m = re.search(r"w(\d+)d(\d+)", name, re.I)
        if m:
            week, day = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2))
        elif len(mp3s) == 1:
            # 只選一個檔案時，才跳出視窗讓你手動輸入
            week = simpledialog.askinteger("週次", "檔名不是 w1d1 格式，請輸入 Week（例如 5）：")
            day = simpledialog.askinteger("天數", "請輸入 Day（1-7）：")
            if week is None or day is None:
                print("✗ 未輸入週次／天數，略過。")
                failed.append(f"{name}（未輸入週次／天數）")
                continue
        else:
            print("✗ 檔名不是 w1d1 格式，略過。")
            failed.append(f"{name}（檔名不是 w1d1 格式）")
            continue

        entry = next((x for x in items if isinstance(x, dict)
                      and x.get("week") == week and x.get("day") == day), None)
        if entry is None:
            print(f"✗ daily.json 裡沒有 Week {week} Day {day}，略過。")
            failed.append(f"{name}（找不到 Week {week} Day {day}）")
            continue
        print(f"目標：Week {week} Day {day}｜{entry.get('tag', '')}")

        try:
            timing, report, total = align(entry["passage"], transcribe(model, mp3), CFG)
        except Exception as e:
            traceback.print_exc()
            print(f"✗ 處理時發生錯誤，略過。")
            failed.append(f"{name}（錯誤：{e}）")
            continue

        print("對齊結果：")
        print("\n".join(report))
        if not timing:
            print("✗ 沒有任何句子對得上，略過（請確認 mp3 內容與英文文稿一致）。")
            failed.append(f"{name}（沒有句子對得上）")
            continue

        entry["timing"] = timing
        done += 1
        print(f"✓ Week {week} Day {day}：{len(timing)} / {total} 個句子")
        if len(timing) < total:
            partial.append(f"{name}（{len(timing)}/{total}）")

    if done == 0:
        msg = "所有檔案都處理失敗，未寫入任何資料。\n\n" + "\n".join(failed)
        print("\n" + msg)
        return messagebox.showerror("沒有可寫入的資料", msg)

    tmp = jpath + ".tmp"
    with open(tmp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(dump_json(data) + "\n")
    os.replace(tmp, jpath)

    msg = f"完成！成功 {done} 個檔案（模型：{MODEL}）。"
    if partial:
        msg += "\n\n有漏句（維護總覽會顯示「部分」），可改用較大的模型（MODEL 改 small.en）或放寬 CFG 再跑一次：\n" + "\n".join(partial)
    if failed:
        msg += "\n\n失敗／略過：\n" + "\n".join(failed)
    print("\n" + msg)
    messagebox.showinfo("完成", msg)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        main()
    except Exception:
        traceback.print_exc()
    input("\n按 Enter 關閉視窗…")
```

### `daily_trash.py`（26.6 KB）
```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""daily_trash.py — Daily 文章「刪除（移到 Trash）」與 Trash 管理（雙擊 daily_trash.bat 使用）

放在網站根目錄（與 daily.json、json_merge.py 同一個資料夾）。

【刪除文章】把某一篇（例如 w1d1）從 daily.json 拿掉，整筆資料與 mp3 先移到 _trash/ 資料夾：
    _trash/w1d1-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS/item.json   整筆資料（含 timing、extra_vocab，原封不動）
                                /w1d1.mp3   音檔（若有）
                                /meta.json  刪除時間、備註、詳解轉移紀錄
  · 刪之前一定先備份 daily.json（backup/）。
  · 這篇擁有的「核心單字以外的詳解（extra_vocab）」：若別篇也有用到那個字，預設轉移到最早用到的那一篇，
    再整理成全題庫只有一份（unify_extra）；沒有別篇用到的，跟著這篇進 Trash。可逐項取消轉移。
  · 位置固定：刪掉後該日變成「此日內容尚未建置」，不重新編號。
  · vocab／words 的音檔是依內容命名的，刪文章不去動它們。

【Trash 管理】還原（放回原本的 week／day，位置已有新文章就拒絕）、永久刪除單筆、清空全部（要輸入 DELETE）。

【版本備份】json_merge.py 合併「文稿補丁」（w1d1_passage.json）時，會先把改之前的整筆資料與舊 mp3 複製到 _trash/w1d1-edit-時間/
    （meta.kind＝'version'）。在 Trash 管理裡它標示「版本備份」；還原＝把舊文稿（passage、timing、edited、review_pending，
    若當時句型被換掉也包含句型）與舊 mp3 放回現有那一篇，不是整篇還原；還原前會先把「目前」的版本也備份一份，所以還原可以再還原。

【trash-index.json】每次變動後重建（網站根目錄）。只放描述資料，不含音檔，網頁的 Trash 清單頁讀它。
【_trash/】內有 mp3，不可被發佈；第一次執行會確認 .gitignore 有 _trash/。
"""
import copy
import importlib.util
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime

try:
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')
except Exception:
    pass

BASE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
MAIN = os.path.join(BASE, 'daily.json')
TRASH = os.path.join(BASE, '_trash')
INDEX = os.path.join(BASE, 'trash-index.json')
DEFAULT_MP3 = 'audio/daily/%s.mp3'


# ───────────────────────── 載入 json_merge.py 的現成函式 ─────────────────────────
def load_jm():
    p = os.path.join(BASE, 'json_merge.py')
    if not os.path.isfile(p):
        sys.exit('找不到 json_merge.py，請把 daily_trash.py 放在它同一個資料夾。')
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('json_merge', p)
    m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    return m


def ask(prompt, default=''):
    try:
        return input(prompt).strip()
    except EOFError:
        return default


def yes(prompt):
    return ask(prompt + ' (y/N)：').lower() in ('y', 'yes')


def parse_id(s):
    """w1d1／W1D1／1 1／1-1 → (1, 1)；D8 專案文章：w1d8-3 → (1, 10)（day 8 是專案第 1 篇、9 是第 2 篇…），也可以直接打 w1d10。看不懂回傳 None。"""
    m = re.match(r'^w?\s*(\d+)\s*[d,\-\s]\s*(\d+)(?:-(\d+))?$', str(s).strip().lower())
    if not m:
        return None
    w, d = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2))
    if m.group(3):
        if d != 8 or int(m.group(3)) < 1:
            return None
        d = 7 + int(m.group(3))
    return (w, d)


def lab(k):
    return 'W%dD8-%d' % (k[0], k[1] - 7) if k[1] >= 8 else 'W%dD%d' % k


def sid(k):
    return 'w%dd8-%d' % (k[0], k[1] - 7) if k[1] >= 8 else 'w%dd%d' % k


def now_stamp():
    return datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')


def safe_path(rel):
    """相對路徑 → 絕對路徑；跑出網站資料夾外就回傳 None。"""
    p = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(BASE, rel))
    return p if p.startswith(BASE + os.sep) else None


def human(n):
    return '%.1f MB' % (n / 1048576.0) if n >= 1048576 else '%.0f KB' % (n / 1024.0)


def write_json(path, obj):
    tmp = path + '.tmp'
    with open(tmp, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        json.dump(obj, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        f.write('\n')
    os.replace(tmp, path)


# ───────────────────────── 主檔 ─────────────────────────
def load_main(jm):
    if not os.path.isfile(MAIN):
        print('找不到 %s' % MAIN)
        return None, None
    data, err = jm.read_json(MAIN)
    items, err2 = jm.split_main(data) if not err else (None, err)
    if err or err2:
        print('daily.json 無法使用：%s' % (err or err2))
        return None, None
    return data, items


def item_key(x):
    return (x.get('week'), x.get('day'))


def find_item(items, k):
    return next((x for x in items if item_key(x) == k), None)


def sorted_items(items):
    return sorted([x for x in items if isinstance(x.get('week'), int) and isinstance(x.get('day'), int)],
                  key=lambda x: (x['week'], x['day']))


# ───────────────────────── .gitignore ─────────────────────────
def ensure_gitignore():
    p = os.path.join(BASE, '.gitignore')
    try:
        text = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read() if os.path.isfile(p) else ''
        if any(l.strip().rstrip('/') == '_trash' or l.strip() == '/_trash/' for l in text.splitlines()):
            return
        with open(p, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(('' if not text or text.endswith('\n') else '\n') + '_trash/\n')
        print('（已在 .gitignore 加入 _trash/，避免 Trash 裡的 mp3 被上傳。若你不是用 GitHub 發佈，請自行確認 _trash 不會被上傳。）\n')
    except Exception as e:
        print('⚠ 無法更新 .gitignore（%s）。請自行確認 _trash/ 不會被發佈。\n' % e)


# ───────────────────────── 詳解轉移 ─────────────────────────
def appears(jm, key, a):
    """這個字（詳解的 text）有沒有出現在 a 的核心單字或文稿（容許 s／es／ed／d／ing 字尾）。"""
    if any(jm.Daily.xkey({'text': v.get('word')}) == key for v in (a.get('vocab') or []) if isinstance(v, dict)):
        return True
    pat = r'(?<![a-z0-9])' + re.escape(key).replace(r'\ ', r'\s+') + r'(?:s|es|ed|d|ing)?(?![a-z0-9])'
    return re.search(pat, str(a.get('passage') or '').lower()) is not None


def plan_extras(jm, items, x):
    """回傳 (rows, dup)；rows＝[{v, key, target(item 或 None)}]，只含「詳解放在這篇」的字；dup＝這篇有、但別篇才是擁有者的字數。"""
    me = item_key(x)
    owners = jm.extra_owners(items)
    others = [a for a in sorted_items(items) if a is not x]
    rows, dup = [], 0
    for v in x.get('extra_vocab') or []:
        k = jm.Daily.xkey(v) if isinstance(v, dict) else ''
        if not k:
            continue
        if owners.get(k) != me:
            dup += 1
            continue
        rows.append({'v': v, 'key': k, 'target': next((a for a in others if appears(jm, k, a)), None)})
    return rows, dup


# ───────────────────────── Trash 資料夾與索引 ─────────────────────────
def trash_folders():
    if not os.path.isdir(TRASH):
        return []
    out = []
    for name in sorted(os.listdir(TRASH)):
        f = os.path.join(TRASH, name)
        if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(f, 'meta.json')) and os.path.isfile(os.path.join(f, 'item.json')):
            out.append(name)
    return out


def read_entry(jm, name):
    f = os.path.join(TRASH, name)
    meta, e1 = jm.read_json(os.path.join(f, 'meta.json'))
    item, e2 = jm.read_json(os.path.join(f, 'item.json'))
    if e1 or e2 or not isinstance(meta, dict) or not isinstance(item, dict):
        return None, None
    return meta, item


def rebuild_index(jm):
    """重建 trash-index.json。實作放在 json_merge.py（文稿補丁的版本備份也用同一份），這裡只是呼叫。"""
    return jm.trash_rebuild_index(BASE)


def run_scan(jm):
    p = os.path.join(BASE, getattr(jm, 'SCAN_NAME', 'Scan總表與音檔.py'))
    if not os.path.isfile(p):
        print('找不到 %s，請之後自行執行。' % os.path.basename(p))
        return
    print('執行 %s …' % os.path.basename(p))
    # 不擷取輸出：讓 Scan 直接印在這個視窗（它在 Windows 用 cp950 輸出，自己擷取再以 UTF-8 解碼會變亂碼）。
    # 它結尾若有「按 Enter」的等待，按一下 Enter 即可回到這裡。
    sys.stdout.flush()
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, p], cwd=BASE)


def offer_scan(jm):
    if yes('\n要接著執行 Scan總表與音檔.py（更新 audio/index.json、vocab_index.json）嗎？'):
        run_scan(jm)
    else:
        print('記得之後執行 Scan總表與音檔.py，網頁才會更新音檔與單字清單。')


# ───────────────────────── 刪除 ─────────────────────────
def collect_files(x, k):
    """這篇相關的音檔：audio/ 底下「檔名以 w3d8 開頭」的檔案（後面不能直接接數字），加上題目 audio 欄位指定的檔。回傳相對路徑（/ 分隔）。"""
    pre, out = sid(k), []
    root = os.path.join(BASE, 'audio')
    rx = re.compile(r'^' + re.escape(pre) + r'(?!\d)', re.I)
    for d, _, fs in os.walk(root):
        for f in sorted(fs):
            if rx.match(f):
                out.append(os.path.relpath(os.path.join(d, f), BASE).replace('\\', '/'))
    a = x.get('audio')
    if a:
        p = safe_path(a)
        if p and os.path.isfile(p):
            rel = os.path.relpath(p, BASE).replace('\\', '/')
            if rel not in out:
                out.append(rel)
    return out


def pick_numbers(s, n):
    return {int(m) - 1 for m in re.findall(r'\d+', s) if 1 <= int(m) <= n}


def delete_flow(jm):
    data, items = load_main(jm)
    if items is None:
        return
    k = parse_id(ask('\n要刪除哪一篇？（例如 w1d1；直接 Enter 取消）：'))
    if not k:
        print('已取消。')
        return
    x = find_item(items, k)
    if not x:
        print('daily.json 裡沒有 %s。' % lab(k))
        return

    files = collect_files(x, k)
    rows, dup = plan_extras(jm, items, x)

    print('\n═══ 影響報告：刪除 %s「%s」 ═══' % (lab(k), x.get('tag') or ''))
    print('  資料：英文 %d 字、單字 %d 個、題目 %d 題、有 timing %s'
          % (len(re.findall(r"[A-Za-z0-9']+", str(x.get('passage') or ''))), len(x.get('vocab') or []),
             len(x.get('questions') or []), '是' if x.get('timing') else '否'))
    print('  詳解（extra_vocab）：這篇擁有 %d 個字%s' % (len(rows), '（另有 %d 個字別篇才是擁有者，不受影響）' % dup if dup else ''))
    for i, r in enumerate(rows, 1):
        t = r['target']
        print('    [%d] %-24s %s' % (i, r['v'].get('text') or r['v'].get('word'),
                                    '→ 預設轉移到 %s（別篇也用到）' % lab(item_key(t)) if t else '→ 沒有別篇用到，預設跟著這篇進 Trash'))

    # ── 詳解：先決定「別篇也用到」的要不要轉移，再決定其餘的要不要保留在 daily.json ──
    cancel = set()
    if any(r['target'] for r in rows):
        s = ask('\n要「取消轉移」的編號（取消後它會跟著這篇進 Trash；空白或逗號分隔；Enter＝全部照預設）：')
        cancel = {i for i in pick_numbers(s, len(rows)) if rows[i]['target']}
    rest = [i for i, r in enumerate(rows) if not r['target'] or i in cancel]
    keep, host = set(), None
    if rest:
        print('\n以下詳解沒有轉移，預設會跟著這篇進 Trash（還原時會一起回來）：')
        for i in rest:
            print('    [%d] %s' % (i + 1, rows[i]['v'].get('text') or rows[i]['v'].get('word')))
        s = ask('要「保留在 daily.json」的編號（可輸入 all；Enter＝都不保留）：')
        keep = set(rest) if s.lower() == 'all' else {i for i in pick_numbers(s, len(rows)) if i in rest}
        if keep:
            prev = [a for a in sorted_items(items) if a is not x and item_key(a) < k]
            nxt = [a for a in sorted_items(items) if a is not x and item_key(a) > k]
            dflt = (prev[-1] if prev else (nxt[0] if nxt else None))
            if not dflt:
                print('沒有其他文章可以放，保留不了，這些詳解會跟著進 Trash。')
                keep = set()
            else:
                s = ask('保留的詳解要掛在哪一篇？（Enter＝%s，或輸入其他篇如 w3d6）：' % lab(item_key(dflt)))
                hk = parse_id(s) if s else None
                host = find_item(items, hk) if hk and hk != k else None
                if s and not host:
                    print('找不到 %s，改用 %s。' % (s, lab(item_key(dflt))))
                host = host or dflt
                print('  （提醒：這些字沒出現在 %s 的文章裡，網頁在那篇不會畫底線，詳解頁與單字總表照常可用。）' % lab(item_key(host)))

    # ── 音檔：列出所有以 w3d8 開頭的檔，確認要移走哪些 ──
    skip = set()
    print('\n音檔：audio/ 底下檔名以「%s」開頭的檔案：' % sid(k))
    if files:
        for i, rel in enumerate(files, 1):
            ap = safe_path(rel)
            print('    [%d] %s（%s）' % (i, rel, human(os.path.getsize(ap)) if ap and os.path.isfile(ap) else '?'))
        s = ask('要「排除、不移走」的編號（Enter＝全部移到 Trash，之後可還原）：')
        skip = pick_numbers(s, len(files))
    else:
        print('    （找不到）')
    move_files = [rel for i, rel in enumerate(files) if i not in skip]

    note = ask('\n備註（可空白，例如刪除原因）：')

    print('\n═══ 最後確認 ═══')
    print('  將從 daily.json 移除：%s「%s」' % (lab(k), x.get('tag') or ''))
    print('  將移到 Trash 的音檔 %d 個%s' % (len(move_files), ''.join('\n    - ' + f for f in move_files)))
    if skip:
        print('  不動的音檔：' + '、'.join(files[i] for i in sorted(skip)))
    n_move = sum(1 for i, r in enumerate(rows) if r['target'] and i not in cancel)
    print('  詳解：轉移 %d 個、保留在 %s %d 個、跟著進 Trash %d 個'
          % (n_move, lab(item_key(host)) if host else '—', len(keep), len(rows) - n_move - len(keep)))
    if not yes('確認執行？'):
        print('已取消，沒有改動任何檔案。')
        return

    moved, kept = [], []
    dest = {}
    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        t = r['target']
        if t and i not in cancel:
            dest[i] = t
        elif i in keep:
            dest[i] = host
        name = r['v'].get('text') or r['v'].get('word')
        (moved.append({'text': name, 'to': sid(item_key(dest[i]))}) if i in dest else kept.append(name))

    # 1) 備份主檔 → 2) 把完整資料與音檔「複製」進 Trash → 3) 寫主檔 → 4) 才移除原音檔（任何一步失敗都不會丟資料）
    bak = jm.backup_main(MAIN, 'daily')
    folder = '%s-%s' % (sid(k), now_stamp())
    fdir = os.path.join(TRASH, folder)
    os.makedirs(fdir, exist_ok=False)
    write_json(os.path.join(fdir, 'item.json'), x)
    for rel in move_files:
        dst = os.path.join(fdir, 'files', *rel.split('/'))
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst), exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copy2(safe_path(rel), dst)
    write_json(os.path.join(fdir, 'meta.json'), {
        'v': 2, 'id': sid(k), 'deleted': datetime.now().isoformat(timespec='seconds'), 'note': note,
        'files': move_files, 'mp3': move_files[0] if move_files else None, 'extra_moved': moved, 'extra_kept': kept,
        'backup': os.path.relpath(bak, BASE).replace('\\', '/')})

    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        t = dest.get(i)
        if t:
            cur = t.setdefault('extra_vocab', [])
            if not any(jm.Daily.xkey(o) == r['key'] for o in cur if isinstance(o, dict)):
                cur.append(copy.deepcopy(r['v']))
    items.remove(x)
    notes = jm.unify_extra(items)
    jm.write_main(MAIN, data)
    for rel in move_files:
        try:
            os.remove(safe_path(rel))
        except OSError as e:
            print('⚠ 已複製到 Trash，但原檔無法刪除（%s），請手動刪除：%s' % (e, rel))
    rebuild_index(jm)

    print('\n✔ 已刪除 %s，資料夾：_trash/%s' % (lab(k), folder))
    print('  daily.json 備份：%s' % os.path.relpath(bak, BASE))
    print('  已移到 Trash 的音檔：%d 個' % len(move_files))
    if moved:
        print('  詳解留在 daily.json：%s' % '、'.join('%s→%s' % (m['text'], m['to'].upper()) for m in moved))
    if kept:
        print('  %d 個詳解跟著這篇進 Trash：%s' % (len(kept), '、'.join(map(str, kept))))
    for n in notes:
        print('  • ' + n)
    print('  提醒：這台瀏覽器裡 %s 的作答／錄音／入庫紀錄還在，重新整理網頁後，在該日的空位頁會看到「保留／清除」提示。' % lab(k))
    offer_scan(jm)


# ───────────────────────── Trash 管理 ─────────────────────────
def nfiles(meta):
    return len(meta.get('files') or []) if meta.get('files') is not None else (1 if meta.get('mp3') else 0)


def show_trash(jm):
    names = trash_folders()
    if not names:
        print('\nTrash 是空的。')
        return []
    print('\n═══ Trash（%d 筆，新的在前）═══' % len(names))
    rows = []
    for n in names:
        meta, item = read_entry(jm, n)
        rows.append((str(meta.get('deleted') if meta else ''), n, meta, item))
    rows.sort(reverse=True)
    out = []
    for i, (_, n, meta, item) in enumerate(rows, 1):
        if not meta:
            print('  [%d] %s（meta／item 讀不到）' % (i, n))
        else:
            ver = meta.get('kind') == 'version'
            print('  [%d] %s%s「%s」 %s於 %s%s%s' % (i, '【版本備份】' if ver else '', lab((item.get('week'), item.get('day'))), item.get('tag') or '',
                                                   '備份' if ver else '刪除', str(meta.get('deleted') or '').replace('T', ' '),
                                                   ('　含音檔 %d 個' % nfiles(meta)) if nfiles(meta) else '',
                                                   '　備註：' + meta['note'] if meta.get('note') else ''))
        out.append(n)
    return out


def restore(jm, name):
    meta, item = read_entry(jm, name)
    if not meta:
        print('這筆的 meta.json／item.json 讀不到，無法還原。')
        return
    if meta.get('kind') == 'version':
        return restore_version(jm, name, meta, item)
    data, items = load_main(jm)
    if items is None:
        return
    k = item_key(item)
    if find_item(items, k):
        print('✖ %s 現在已經有文章了（%s），無法還原。\n  請先把現有那篇刪除（移到 Trash），或維持現狀。' % (lab(k), find_item(items, k).get('tag') or ''))
        return
    # 音檔：新格式 meta['files']（相對路徑，存在 Trash 的 files/ 底下）；舊格式 meta['mp3']（直接放在資料夾內）
    plan_files = []
    if meta.get('files') is not None:
        for rel in meta.get('files') or []:
            plan_files.append((os.path.join(TRASH, name, 'files', *rel.split('/')), safe_path(rel), rel))
    elif meta.get('mp3'):
        plan_files.append((os.path.join(TRASH, name, os.path.basename(meta['mp3'])), safe_path(meta['mp3']), meta['mp3']))
    ok_files, overwrite = [], None
    for src, dst, rel in plan_files:
        if not dst or not os.path.isfile(src):
            print('⚠ Trash 裡找不到 %s，略過。' % rel)
        elif os.path.isfile(dst):
            if overwrite is None:
                overwrite = yes('%s 等音檔已存在，要用 Trash 裡的版本覆蓋嗎？' % rel)
            if overwrite:
                ok_files.append((src, dst))
        else:
            ok_files.append((src, dst))
    if not yes('確認還原 %s「%s」？' % (lab(k), item.get('tag') or '')):
        print('已取消。')
        return
    bak = jm.backup_main(MAIN, 'daily')
    items.append(item)
    items.sort(key=lambda a: (a.get('week') if isinstance(a.get('week'), int) else 0, a.get('day') if isinstance(a.get('day'), int) else 0))
    notes = jm.unify_extra(items)
    jm.write_main(MAIN, data)
    for src, dst in ok_files:
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst), exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copy2(src, dst)
    shutil.rmtree(os.path.join(TRASH, name))
    rebuild_index(jm)
    print('✔ 已還原 %s（daily.json 備份：%s）' % (lab(k), os.path.relpath(bak, BASE)))
    for n in notes:
        print('  • ' + n)
    offer_scan(jm)


def restore_version(jm, name, meta, item):
    """還原「文稿版本備份」：把舊文稿（與舊 timing、舊 mp3）放回現有那一篇。先備份目前的版本，所以可以再還原回來。"""
    data, items = load_main(jm)
    if items is None:
        return
    k = item_key(item)
    cur = find_item(items, k)
    if not cur:
        print('✖ %s 現在不在 daily.json（可能已被刪除），無法還原文稿版本。\n  若要找回整篇，請還原刪除時留下的 Trash 項目。' % lab(k))
        return
    fields = [f for f in (meta.get('fields') or ['passage', 'timing', 'edited', 'review_pending'])]
    words = lambda t: len(re.findall(r"[A-Za-z0-9']+", str(t or '')))
    print('\n還原 %s 的舊版文稿：' % lab(k))
    print('  目前：英文 %d 字，timing %d 段%s' % (words(cur.get('passage')), len(cur.get('timing') or []), '，待檢查（review_pending）' if cur.get('review_pending') else ''))
    print('  舊版：英文 %d 字，timing %d 段，備份於 %s' % (words(item.get('passage')), len(item.get('timing') or []), str(meta.get('deleted') or '').replace('T', ' ')))
    print('  會換回的欄位：%s' % '、'.join(fields))
    print('  注意：補丁之後若用「修正補丁」改過翻譯、單字、題目，那些不會被還原（它們不在這份備份裡）。')
    plan_files = [(os.path.join(TRASH, name, 'files', *rel.split('/')), safe_path(rel), rel) for rel in (meta.get('files') or [])]
    plan_files = [(a, b, c) for a, b, c in plan_files if b and os.path.isfile(a)]
    if plan_files:
        print('  音檔：%s（現有同名檔會被取代；取代前已先備份在新的版本備份裡）' % '、'.join(c for _, _, c in plan_files))
    if not yes('確認還原？'):
        print('已取消。')
        return
    bak = jm.backup_main(MAIN, 'daily')
    jm.version_backup(BASE, copy.deepcopy(cur), fields, '還原前的版本')   # 目前的版本（含目前的 mp3）也留一份
    for f in fields:
        if f in item:
            cur[f] = copy.deepcopy(item[f])
        else:
            cur.pop(f, None)
    jm.write_main(MAIN, data)
    for src, dst, _ in plan_files:
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst), exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copy2(src, dst)
    shutil.rmtree(os.path.join(TRASH, name))
    rebuild_index(jm)
    print('✔ 已還原 %s 的舊文稿（daily.json 備份：%s）' % (lab(k), os.path.relpath(bak, BASE)))
    print('  還原前的版本已另存在 Trash（版本備份）。')
    offer_scan(jm)


def purge(jm, name):
    meta, item = read_entry(jm, name)
    print('永久刪除 _trash/%s（資料與 mp3 都會消失，無法復原）。' % name)
    if yes('確定？'):
        shutil.rmtree(os.path.join(TRASH, name))
        rebuild_index(jm)
        print('✔ 已永久刪除。')
    else:
        print('已取消。')


def trash_menu(jm):
    while True:
        names = show_trash(jm)
        if not names:
            rebuild_index(jm)
            return
        s = ask('\n輸入 r 編號（或 r w3d8）＝還原　x 編號＝永久刪除　clear＝清空全部　Enter＝回主選單：').lower()
        if not s:
            return
        if s == 'clear':
            if ask('這會永久刪除 Trash 內全部 %d 筆。請輸入 DELETE 確認：' % len(names)) == 'DELETE':
                for n in names:
                    shutil.rmtree(os.path.join(TRASH, n), ignore_errors=True)
                rebuild_index(jm)
                print('✔ 已清空 Trash。')
            else:
                print('已取消。')
            continue
        m = re.match(r'^([rx])\s*(.*)$', s)
        pick = None
        if m:
            arg = m.group(2).strip()
            if not arg and len(names) == 1:
                pick = names[0]                                   # 只有一筆：單打 r／x 就好
            elif arg.isdigit() and 1 <= int(arg) <= len(names):
                pick = names[int(arg) - 1]                        # r 1
            elif parse_id(arg):                                   # r w3d8（有多版時取最新的一版；刪除的優先，版本備份請用編號）
                hit = [n for n in names if n.startswith(sid(parse_id(arg)) + '-')]
                pick = next((n for n in hit if '-edit-' not in n), hit[0] if hit else None)
        if not pick:
            ex = 'r' if len(names) == 1 else 'r 1'
            _m = re.match(r'^(w\d+d\d+(?:-\d+)?)-(?:\d{8}|edit)', names[0])
            print('看不懂。請輸入像「%s」、「x 1」或「r %s」（編號或文章 id 都可以）。' % (ex, _m.group(1) if _m else 'w1d1'))
            continue
        (restore if m.group(1) == 'r' else purge)(jm, pick)


def main():
    jm = load_jm()
    print('Daily 文章 Trash 工具（網站資料夾：%s）\n' % BASE)
    ensure_gitignore()
    rebuild_index(jm)
    while True:
        s = ask('\n1＝刪除文章（移到 Trash）　2＝Trash 管理（還原／永久刪除）　Enter＝離開：')
        if s == '1':
            delete_flow(jm)
        elif s == '2':
            trash_menu(jm)
        elif not s:
            return
        else:
            print('請輸入 1、2，或直接按 Enter 離開。')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### `daily_trash.bat`（0.1 KB）
```bat
@echo off
chcp 65001 >nul
cd /d "%~dp0"
where py >nul 2>nul && (py daily_trash.py) || (python daily_trash.py)
echo.
pause
```

### `Scan總表與音檔.py`（18.2 KB）
```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Scan總表與音檔.py — 掃描 audio 資料夾，產生 audio/index.json（本檔放在網站根目錄，與 index.html 同層，雙擊執行）

專案結構：
  index.html / part2.html / daily.html / *.json   ← 網站根目錄（ROOT）
  Scan總表與音檔.py                               ← 本檔（與 index.html 同層）
  audio/index.json                                ← 本檔產生
  audio/p1/、audio/p2/、audio/p3/、audio/p4/、audio/p5/、audio/p6/、audio/p7/、audio/daily/   ← mp3

預期檔案（由題庫 json 推算）：
  Part 1  audio/p1/{id}-s01.mp3 … -s12.mp3          （part1.json，每題 pool 幾句就幾個檔）
  Part 2  audio/p2/{id}-q.mp3 與 {id}-s01.mp3 …      （part2.json）
  Part 3  audio/p3/{id}-s01.mp3 …                   （part3.json，每句對話一檔，依 dialogue 句數）
  Part 4  audio/p4/{id}.mp3                          （part4.json，整段獨白一個檔，不分句）
  Part 5  audio/p5/{id}.mp3                         （part5.json，一題一檔，朗讀答案填入的完整句；say 欄位若有則朗讀 say）
  Part 6  audio/p6/{id}.mp3                         （part6.json，一篇一檔，朗讀答案填入的整篇；say 欄位若有則朗讀 say）
  Part 7  audio/p7/{id}-d{k}.mp3                    （part7.json，每份需朗讀的文件一檔，k 從 1 起＝docs 順序；form/invoice/schedule/table 或 read:false 的文件不需要）
  Daily   audio/daily/w1d1.mp3、D8 專案 w1d8-1.mp3（或題目的 audio 欄位）  （daily.json，整篇一個檔）
  字母    audio/A.mp3 … E.mp3（Part 1／2 共用，大寫檔名；Part 1 需 A–D、Part 2 需 A–C 到齊，才會在每個選項前先念字母）
整題到齊才列入 complete；網頁只對 complete 的題目用 mp3。
Timing 狀態：依 daily.json 每篇的 passage 與 timing 欄位，判斷 完成／部分／異常／未做，寫進 audio/index.json 的 timing 區塊
  （首頁維護總覽直接讀，不必再下載整份 daily.json），並列出哪幾句沒對到。
網站檔案清單：把根目錄的檔案清單寫成 site_files.json，讓 index 的專案地圖找出沒被任何檔案提到的檔案。
TSL 單字總表：比對 daily.json 的單字（vocab、extra_vocab 的單字與詞形），把「總表中已收錄的字」與「有沒有詳解」寫成小檔
  vocab_index.json（網站根目錄）。vocab.html 只讀這個小檔，不必每次下載整份 daily.json。需要根目錄有 tsl.js。
另外檢查：孤兒檔（不屬於任何題目，多半是打錯檔名）、檔名大小寫不符（GitHub Pages 區分大小寫）、0 KB 空檔、
舊位置的 daily 檔（audio/w1d1.mp3 → 請搬到 audio/daily/）。
"""
import json, os, re, time, unicodedata

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))  # 網站根目錄（本檔所在，題庫 json 也在這）
AUD = os.path.join(ROOT, 'audio')                  # audio 資料夾
warn = []


def did(week, day):
    """文章 id：D8 是專案，day 8、9、10… ＝ 專案內第 1、2、3 篇 → w1d8-1、w1d8-2…；其他天 w1d3。"""
    return 'w%sd8-%d' % (week, int(day) - 7) if int(day) >= 8 else 'w%sd%s' % (week, day)


def items(fn):
    p = os.path.join(ROOT, fn)
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        print('找不到 %s，略過' % fn)
        return []
    with open(p, encoding='utf-8-sig') as fh:
        j = json.load(fh)
    return j if isinstance(j, list) else j.get('items', [])


def listdir(d):
    return sorted(f for f in os.listdir(d) if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(d, f))) if os.path.isdir(d) else []


def check(d, expected, label):
    """expected: {id: [檔名.mp3,...]} → (complete, partial, orphans)"""
    have = listdir(d)
    low = {f.lower(): f for f in have}
    allexp = set()
    complete, partial = [], {}
    for id_, names in expected.items():
        miss = []
        for n in names:
            allexp.add(n)
            if n in have:
                if os.path.getsize(os.path.join(d, n)) == 0:
                    warn.append('%s/%s 是 0 KB 空檔' % (label, n))
                    miss.append(n)
            else:
                miss.append(n)
                if n.lower() in low:
                    warn.append('%s：檔名大小寫不符，現有「%s」，應為「%s」' % (label, low[n.lower()], n))
        if not miss:
            complete.append(id_)
        elif len(miss) < len(names):
            partial[id_] = [m[:-4] for m in miss]
    orphans = [f for f in have if f not in allexp and f.lower().endswith('.mp3')]
    return complete, partial, orphans


out = {'v': int(time.time())}
TOT = {}   # 各 Part 的題目總數，給首頁「維護總覽」用

# 選項字母 audio/A.mp3 … E.mp3（Part 1／Part 2 共用，大寫檔名）
have_root = listdir(AUD)
low_root = {f.lower(): f for f in have_root}
letters = []
for c in 'ABCDE':
    n = c + '.mp3'
    if n in have_root:
        if os.path.getsize(os.path.join(AUD, n)) == 0:
            warn.append('audio/%s 是 0 KB 空檔' % n)
        else:
            letters.append(c)
    elif n.lower() in low_root:
        warn.append('audio：字母檔名大小寫不符，現有「%s」，應為「%s」' % (low_root[n.lower()], n))
out['letters'] = letters
print('letters：已有 %s（Part 1 需 A–D，Part 2 需 A–C）' % (''.join(letters) or '無'))
for part, fn, with_q in (('p1', 'part1.json', False), ('p2', 'part2.json', True)):
    exp = {}
    for it in items(fn):
        if not isinstance(it, dict) or not it.get('id') or not isinstance(it.get('pool'), list):
            continue
        i = it['id']
        exp[i] = ([i + '-q.mp3'] if with_q else []) + ['%s-s%02d.mp3' % (i, k + 1) for k in range(len(it['pool']))]
    c, p, o = check(os.path.join(AUD, part), exp, 'audio/' + part)
    out[part] = {'complete': c, 'partial': p, 'orphans': o}
    TOT[part] = len(exp)
    print('%s：題目 %d，音檔完整 %d，部分 %d，孤兒檔 %d' % (part, len(exp), len(c), len(p), len(o)))

# Part 3：audio/p3/{id}-s01.mp3 …（每句對話一檔；網頁目前只用對話音檔，題目與選項仍不念）
exp = {}
for it in items('part3.json'):
    if isinstance(it, dict) and it.get('id') and isinstance(it.get('dialogue'), list):
        exp[it['id']] = ['%s-s%02d.mp3' % (it['id'], k + 1) for k in range(len(it['dialogue']))]
c, p, o = check(os.path.join(AUD, 'p3'), exp, 'audio/p3')
out['p3'] = {'complete': c, 'partial': p, 'orphans': o}
TOT['p3'] = len(exp)
print('p3：題組 %d，音檔完整 %d，部分 %d，孤兒檔 %d' % (len(exp), len(c), len(p), len(o)))

# Part 4：audio/p4/{id}.mp3（整段獨白一個檔；單一說話者，不分句）
exp = {}
for it in items('part4.json'):
    if isinstance(it, dict) and it.get('id') and isinstance(it.get('script'), list):
        exp[it['id']] = ['%s.mp3' % it['id']]
c, p, o = check(os.path.join(AUD, 'p4'), exp, 'audio/p4')
legacy = [f for f in o if re.match(r'^d\d-\d{3}-[emh]-s\d+\.mp3$', f)]
if legacy:
    warn.append('audio/p4 有 %d 個舊的逐句檔（如 %s）。Part 4 已改為整段獨白一個檔 {id}.mp3，舊檔可刪除或另存' % (len(legacy), legacy[0]))
    o = [f for f in o if f not in legacy]
out['p4'] = {'complete': c, 'partial': p, 'orphans': o}
TOT['p4'] = len(exp)
print('p4：題組 %d，音檔完整 %d，孤兒檔 %d' % (len(exp), len(c), len(o)))

# Part 5：audio/p5/{id}.mp3（一題一檔，朗讀答案填入的完整句；partial 固定為空，保留是為了與其他 Part 相同的讀取方式）
exp = {}
for it in items('part5.json'):
    if isinstance(it, dict) and it.get('id') and isinstance(it.get('sentence'), dict):
        exp[it['id']] = [it['id'] + '.mp3']
c, p, o = check(os.path.join(AUD, 'p5'), exp, 'audio/p5')
out['p5'] = {'complete': c, 'partial': {}, 'orphans': o}
TOT['p5'] = len(exp)
print('p5：題目 %d，音檔完整 %d，缺 %d，孤兒檔 %d' % (len(exp), len(c), len(exp) - len(c), len(o)))

# Part 6：audio/p6/{id}.mp3（一篇一檔，朗讀答案填入的整篇；say 欄位若有則朗讀 say）
exp = {}
for it in items('part6.json'):
    if isinstance(it, dict) and it.get('id') and isinstance(it.get('body'), list):
        exp[it['id']] = [it['id'] + '.mp3']
c, p, o = check(os.path.join(AUD, 'p6'), exp, 'audio/p6')
out['p6'] = {'complete': c, 'partial': {}, 'orphans': o}
TOT['p6'] = len(exp)
print('p6：文章 %d，音檔完整 %d，缺 %d，孤兒檔 %d' % (len(exp), len(c), len(exp) - len(c), len(o)))

# Part 7：audio/p7/{id}-d{k}.mp3（每份需朗讀的文件一檔；k 從 1 起＝docs 順序）
#   與 part7.js 的規則一致：kind 為 form／invoice／schedule／table，或 read 為 false 的文件不朗讀，不需要音檔。
#   p7.complete 列的是「文件鍵 {id}-d{k}」（不是題組 id），part7.js 也是這樣讀；total 以文件數計。
NOAUD7 = ('form', 'invoice', 'schedule', 'table')
exp = {}
for it in items('part7.json'):
    if isinstance(it, dict) and it.get('id') and isinstance(it.get('docs'), list):
        for k, d in enumerate(it['docs']):
            if isinstance(d, dict) and d.get('read') is not False and d.get('kind') not in NOAUD7:
                key = '%s-d%d' % (it['id'], k + 1)
                exp[key] = [key + '.mp3']
c, p, o = check(os.path.join(AUD, 'p7'), exp, 'audio/p7')
out['p7'] = {'complete': c, 'partial': {}, 'orphans': o, 'unit': '份文件'}
TOT['p7'] = len(exp)
print('p7：需朗讀的文件 %d，音檔完整 %d，缺 %d，孤兒檔 %d' % (len(exp), len(c), len(exp) - len(c), len(o)))

# Daily：audio/daily/{id}.mp3；題目若有 audio 欄位（相對網站根目錄的路徑）則以該路徑為準
DDIR = os.path.join(AUD, 'daily')
dexp, dcustom = {}, {}
for it in items('daily.json'):
    if isinstance(it, dict) and 'week' in it and 'day' in it:
        i = did(it['week'], it['day'])
        a = it.get('audio')
        if a and os.path.normpath(os.path.join(ROOT, a)) != os.path.normpath(os.path.join(DDIR, i + '.mp3')):
            dcustom[i] = a                      # 自訂路徑，只檢查檔案存在
        else:
            dexp[i] = [i + '.mp3']
dc, dp, do = check(DDIR, dexp, 'audio/daily')
for i, a in dcustom.items():
    f = os.path.join(ROOT, a)
    if os.path.isfile(f) and os.path.getsize(f) > 0:
        dc.append(i)
    else:
        warn.append('daily %s 的 audio 欄位指向 %s，但檔案不存在或是 0 KB' % (i, a))
# 舊位置（audio/w1d1.mp3）提醒搬家
for f in listdir(AUD):
    base = f[:-4] if f.lower().endswith('.mp3') else None
    if base and base in dexp:
        warn.append('audio/%s 還在舊位置，請搬到 audio/daily/' % f)
out['daily'] = {'complete': dc, 'partial': dp, 'orphans': do}
TOT['daily'] = len(dexp) + len(dcustom)
print('daily：題目 %d，音檔完整 %d，孤兒檔 %d' % (len(dexp) + len(dcustom), len(dc), len(do)))

for part in ('p1', 'p2', 'p3', 'p4', 'p5', 'p6', 'p7', 'daily'):
    for o in out[part]['orphans']:
        warn.append('audio/%s/%s 不屬於任何題目（檔名打錯？）' % (part, o))
    out[part]['total'] = TOT.get(part, 0)
# ===== Timing 狀態（寫進 audio/index.json 的 "timing"）=====
#   切句規則與 daily_timestamps.py 的 split_sentences 相同（兩邊要一起改）。
#   完成 ok＝每一句都有 timing；部分 partial＝有 timing 但有句子沒對到；
#   異常 bad＝timing 的句子範圍對不上現在的文稿（文稿改過？）或時間順序不對；未做 none＝沒有 timing。
_ABBR = {"mr", "ms", "mrs", "dr", "inc", "co", "ltd", "no", "vs", "st", "jr", "sr"}


def split_sentences(text):
    res, pos = [], 0

    def add(base, a, b, line):
        seg = line[a:b]
        lead = len(seg) - len(seg.lstrip())
        seg = seg.strip()
        if seg:
            res.append((base + a + lead, base + a + lead + len(seg)))
    for line in text.split("\n"):
        seg_start = 0
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?<=[.!?])\s+(?=[A-Z"\'(])', line):
            words = line[seg_start:m.start()].split()
            last = words[-1] if words else ""
            if last.rstrip(".").lower() in _ABBR or re.fullmatch(r"(?:[A-Za-z]\.){2,}", last):
                continue
            add(pos, seg_start, m.start(), line)
            seg_start = m.end()
        add(pos, seg_start, len(line), line)
        pos += len(line) + 1
    return res


def timing_state(it):
    passage = it.get('passage') or ''
    tm = it.get('timing')
    sents = split_sentences(passage)
    if not isinstance(tm, list) or not tm:
        return 'none', '尚未對時'
    sset = set(sents)
    got = []
    for e in tm:
        if not isinstance(e, dict) or not all(k in e for k in ('start', 'end', 'from', 'to')):
            return 'bad', 'timing 格式不完整'
        got.append((e['from'], e['to']))
    if any(g not in sset for g in got):
        return 'bad', 'timing 的句子範圍與現在的文稿對不上（文稿改過？請重新對時）'
    if any(not (e['start'] < e['end']) for e in tm) or any(tm[i]['start'] < tm[i - 1]['start'] for i in range(1, len(tm))):
        return 'bad', 'timing 時間順序異常'
    gset = set(got)
    miss = [i + 1 for i, s in enumerate(sents) if s not in gset]
    if miss:
        return 'partial', '只涵蓋 %d/%d 句，缺第 %s 句' % (len(sents) - len(miss), len(sents), '、'.join(map(str, miss)))
    return 'ok', ''


_t = {'total': 0, 'ok': 0, 'partial': 0, 'bad': 0, 'none': 0, 'todo': []}
for it in items('daily.json'):
    if not isinstance(it, dict) or 'week' not in it or 'day' not in it:
        continue
    s, why = timing_state(it)
    _t['total'] += 1
    _t[s] += 1
    if s != 'ok':
        _t['todo'].append({'id': did(it['week'], it['day']), 'tag': it.get('tag', ''), 's': s, 'why': why})
out['timing'] = _t
print('timing：共 %d 篇，完成 %d，部分 %d，異常 %d，未做 %d' % (_t['total'], _t['ok'], _t['partial'], _t['bad'], _t['none']))
for x in _t['todo']:
    print('  %s %s：%s' % (x['id'], {'partial': '部分', 'bad': '異常', 'none': '未做'}[x['s']], x['why']))

out['warn'] = warn   # 首頁「維護總覽」會顯示這些警告

os.makedirs(AUD, exist_ok=True)
with open(os.path.join(AUD, 'index.json'), 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    json.dump(out, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
print('\n已寫入 audio/index.json')
for w in warn:
    print('⚠', w)
# ===== TSL 單字總表索引：vocab_index.json（網站根目錄）=====
#   做法與 index.json 相同：先在本機掃描好，網頁只讀結果。
#   比對對象：daily.json 每篇的 vocab[].word、extra_vocab 的單字（type=word）及其 forms[].w；
#   拼字比對（小寫、去重音符號），不推測詞形變化。
def norm(s):
    s = unicodedata.normalize('NFD', str(s or '').lower())
    return ''.join(c for c in s if unicodedata.category(c) != 'Mn').strip()

tsl_path = os.path.join(ROOT, 'tsl.js')
tsl = None
if os.path.exists(tsl_path):
    m = re.search(r'TSL_WORDS\s*=\s*\("(.*?)"\)\.split', open(tsl_path, encoding='utf-8').read(), re.S)
    if m:
        tsl = [norm(w) for w in m.group(1).split(',') if w.strip()]
if not tsl:
    print('\n找不到 tsl.js 或格式不符，略過 vocab_index.json（請把 tsl.js 放在網站根目錄）')
else:
    tset = set(tsl)
    # learned：字 → [週, 天, 詳解狀態]
    #   詳解狀態 2＝有自己的 extra_vocab 詳解；1＝只出現在別的字詳解的 forms 裡（詳解含在那個字裡）；
    #   0＝只有核心單字卡（vocab），還沒有生成詳解。
    learned = {}
    # 任何一篇有詳解就算有（同一個字在別篇入庫時，網頁沿用同一份詳解）
    all_items = [it for it in items('daily.json') if isinstance(it, dict) and 'week' in it and 'day' in it]
    g_own, g_via = set(), set()
    for it in all_items:
        for e in it.get('extra_vocab') or []:
            if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get('type', 'word') == 'word':
                g_own.add(norm(e.get('text')))
                for f in e.get('forms') or []:
                    if isinstance(f, dict):
                        g_via.add(norm(f.get('w')))

    def add(word, w, d, s):
        k = norm(word)
        if k not in tset:
            return
        # 核心單字缺詳解（s=0）優先顯示，才不會被其他文章的 forms 蓋掉
        if k not in learned or (s == 0 and learned[k][2] != 0):
            learned[k] = [w, d, s]
    for it in items('daily.json'):
        if not isinstance(it, dict) or 'week' not in it or 'day' not in it:
            continue
        w, d = it['week'], it['day']
        extras = [e for e in (it.get('extra_vocab') or []) if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get('type', 'word') == 'word']
        own, via = g_own, g_via
        for v in it.get('vocab') or []:
            if isinstance(v, dict):
                k = norm(v.get('word'))
                add(v.get('word'), w, d, 2 if k in own else 1 if k in via else 0)
        for e in extras:
            add(e.get('text'), w, d, 2)
            for f in e.get('forms') or []:
                if isinstance(f, dict):
                    add(f.get('w'), w, d, 1)
    gap = sorted((v[0], v[1], k) for k, v in learned.items() if v[2] == 0)
    vi = {'v': int(time.time()), 'total': len(tsl), 'have': len(learned), 'nodetail': len(gap), 'tsl': dict(sorted(learned.items()))}
    print('\nTSL 已收錄但缺詳解：%d 字' % len(gap))
    for w_, d_, k_ in gap:
        print('  %s  %s' % (did(w_, d_), k_))
    with open(os.path.join(ROOT, 'vocab_index.json'), 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        json.dump(vi, f, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':'))
    print('\nTSL 單字總表：已收錄 %d / %d 字，還缺 %d 字 → 已寫入 vocab_index.json' % (len(learned), len(tsl), len(tsl) - len(learned)))

# ===== 網站檔案清單 site_files.json（給 index 維護總覽的「專案地圖」找沒用到的檔案）=====
_skip = {'.git', '.vscode', '__pycache__', 'node_modules'}
_sf = {'v': int(time.time()), 'files': [], 'dirs': {}}
for _f in sorted(os.listdir(ROOT)):
    _p = os.path.join(ROOT, _f)
    if os.path.isfile(_p):
        _sf['files'].append({'n': _f, 'kb': round(os.path.getsize(_p) / 1024, 1), 't': int(os.path.getmtime(_p))})
    elif os.path.isdir(_p) and _f not in _skip and not _f.startswith('.'):
        _sf['dirs'][_f] = sum(len(fs) for _, _, fs in os.walk(_p))
with open(os.path.join(ROOT, 'site_files.json'), 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    json.dump(_sf, f, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':'))
print('網站檔案清單：%d 個檔案、%d 個資料夾 → 已寫入 site_files.json' % (len(_sf['files']), len(_sf['dirs'])))

try:
    input('\n按 Enter 結束')
except EOFError:
    pass
```

```
（骨架：檔案共 1716 行；列出模組說明、常數、class／def 與其說明第一行）
模組說明：
  auto-speech.py  ——  補錄音檔（Part 1～Part 7、Daily 共用）：勾選要補錄的項目，產生缺少的 mp3
  
  用法：把這支檔案放在「有 part1.json…part7.json、daily.json 的資料夾」（或 audio 資料夾裡），直接雙擊執行。
    ・雙擊：先跳出「選擇題庫」視窗（會顯示各題庫還缺幾個檔），選一個進入
    ・指定題庫：python auto-speech.py part3        （也可寫 3、p3、photo、daily）
    ・json_merge.py 合併完成後，會自動用這個方式啟動對應的題庫
```

```json
   "w6d6",
   "w6d7",
   "w1d8-6",
   "w7d1",
   "w1d8-7"
  ],
  "partial": {},
  "orphans": [],
  "total": 50
 },
 "timing": {
  "total": 50,
  "ok": 50,
  "partial": 0,
  "bad": 0,
  "none": 0,
  "todo": []
 },
 "warn": []
}
```

### `vocab.html`（18.0 KB）
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-Hant" class="">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>TSL 單字總表 · TOEIC Daily Coach</title>
<script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
<script>tailwind.config={darkMode:'class'}</script>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="style.css">
<script src="cloud-sync.js"></script>
<script>
/* 字級：4 段（小／標準／大／特大），存在這台裝置（不跨裝置同步）。第一次使用時手機預設「大」 */
var FONT_LV=[14,16,18,20],FONT_NM=['小','標準','大','特大'];
function fontGet(){var v=parseInt(localStorage.getItem('dailyfont'));if(v>=0&&v<FONT_LV.length)return v;return window.matchMedia&&matchMedia('(max-width:767px)').matches?2:1}
function fontApply(){document.documentElement.style.fontSize=FONT_LV[fontGet()]+'px'}
function fontCycle(){var n=(fontGet()+1)%FONT_LV.length;try{localStorage.setItem('dailyfont',n)}catch(e){}fontApply();
  if(typeof dnToast==='function')dnToast('字級：'+FONT_NM[n]);
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-fontlbl]').forEach(function(e){e.textContent=FONT_NM[n]});
  if(typeof render==='function')try{render()}catch(e){}}
fontApply();
</script>
</head>
<body class="bg-slate-50 text-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:text-slate-200">
<div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto p-3 md:p-8 pb-16">
  <header class="mb-4">
    <a href="index.html" class="text-xs text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 hover:underline">← 回首頁</a>
    <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><h1 class="text-2xl font-bold mt-1">📚 TSL 單字總表</h1><button onclick="fontCycle()" class="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm shrink-0" aria-label="調整字級">Aa <span data-fontlbl class="text-xs text-slate-500"></span></button></div>
    <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">New TOEIC Service List 1.2 · 1250 字 · 可依 D1–D7 主題篩選 · 依字母分組，點字母展開；已收錄的字可點開詳解</p>
  </header>

  <div id="sum" class="mb-4"></div>

  <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-3 text-sm">
    <div class="flex rounded-lg border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 overflow-hidden" id="fsc"></div>
    <div class="flex rounded-lg border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 overflow-hidden" id="fs"></div>
    <div class="flex rounded-lg border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 overflow-hidden" id="fb"></div>
    <input id="q" type="search" placeholder="搜尋單字…" class="flex-1 min-w-[8rem] rounded-lg border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm">
    <button data-all="1" class="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 px-3 py-1.5 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800">全部展開</button>
    <button data-all="0" class="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 px-3 py-1.5 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800">全部收合</button>
  </div>

  <div id="fdw" class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 mb-3 text-xs"></div>

  <div id="list" class="space-y-2"></div>
  <p id="src" class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-4"></p>

  <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-6 leading-relaxed">
    資料來源：Browne, C. &amp; Culligan, B. <i>The TOEIC Service List</i> 1.2（<a class="underline" href="https://www.newgeneralservicelist.com/toeic-service-list" target="_blank" rel="noopener">newgeneralservicelist.com</a>），CC BY-SA 4.0。
    數字為官方詞頻排名；圓點顏色是依官方分級（1–400／401–800／801–1250）換算的預估分數帶，非官方分數。
    比對為拼字比對（含補充詳解的詞形），不推測其他詞形變化。
  </p>
</div>

<script src="tsl.js"></script>
<script>
const KEY = 'toeicCoachV2';
let S = {}; try { S = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) || '{}'); } catch (e) {}
document.documentElement.classList.toggle('dark', !!S.dark);

const norm = s => String(s || '').toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').trim();
const esc = t => String(t).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c]));

const BANDS = [
  { k: 1, max: 400, label: '預估 <650', dot: 'bg-emerald-500', cls: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-300' },
  { k: 2, max: 800, label: '預估 650–749', dot: 'bg-amber-500', cls: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-950 dark:text-amber-300' },
  { k: 3, max: 1250, label: '預估 ≥750', dot: 'bg-rose-500', cls: 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 dark:bg-rose-950 dark:text-rose-300' }
];
const bandOf = r => BANDS.find(b => r <= b.max);

/* 資料：依字母排序後分組（rank 保留官方排名） */
const ROWS = TSL_WORDS.map((w, i) => ({ w, n: norm(w), r: i + 1, b: bandOf(i + 1), d: TSL_CAT[i][0], d2: TSL_CAT[i][1] || '' })).sort((a, b) => a.n.localeCompare(b.n));
const LETTERS = [...new Set(ROWS.map(x => x.n[0].toUpperCase()))];

let IX = null, OTHERS = [], OTH_LOADED = false;
TSL_DNAME.N = '非 TSL';
const TSLSET = new Set(TSL_WORDS.map(norm));
let LEARNED = new Map(); // norm word -> { w, d, s }　s＝詳解狀態：2 有詳解、1 含在其他字詳解內、0 缺詳解、undefined 未知（舊版 index）
const gap = x => { if (x.o) return x.s === 0; const h = LEARNED.get(x.n); return !!h && h.s === 0; };
const qidWD = q => { const m = /^w(\d+)d(\d+)$/.exec(q || ''); return m ? [+m[1], +m[2]] : [0, 0]; };
/* 入庫但還沒寫回 daily.json 的字（存在這個瀏覽器的 S.marks）：TSL 單字 → 算「已收錄、缺詳解」 */
function mergeMarks() {
  (S.marks || []).forEach(m => {
    const k = norm(m.text); if (!k || m.type !== 'word' || !TSLSET.has(k) || LEARNED.has(k)) return;
    const [w, d] = qidWD(m.qid); LEARNED.set(k, { w, d, s: 0 });
  });
}
/* 「我的入庫」要列的非 TSL 字／片語：優先讀 vocab_index.json 的 other，沒有才讀 daily.json 的 extra_vocab，再加上尚未寫回的入庫項目 */
async function ensureOthers() {
  if (OTH_LOADED) return; OTH_LOADED = true;
  let list = [];
  if (IX && Array.isArray(IX.other)) list = IX.other.map(a => ({ text: a[0], type: a[1], from: 'W' + a[2] + 'D' + a[3], s: a[4] }));
  else { const j = await getJ('daily.json'); if (j) (Array.isArray(j) ? j : (j.items || [])).forEach(x => (x.extra_vocab || []).forEach(e => { const t = e.text || e.word; if (t) list.push({ text: t, type: e.type || 'word', from: 'W' + x.week + 'D' + x.day, s: 2 }); })); }
  (S.marks || []).forEach(m => { if (m.text) { const [w, d] = qidWD(m.qid); list.push({ text: m.text, type: m.type || 'word', from: w ? 'W' + w + 'D' + d : '', s: 0, pend: 1 }); } });
  const seen = new Set(); OTHERS = [];
  list.forEach(o => {
    const k = norm(o.text); if (!k || seen.has(k)) return;
    if (TSLSET.has(k) && (o.type || 'word') === 'word') return; // TSL 的字已在上面的總表
    seen.add(k); OTHERS.push({ o: 1, w: o.text, n: k, r: null, b: null, d: 'N', d2: '', type: o.type || 'word', s: o.s, from: o.from });
  });
}
const typeTag = t => t === 'phrase' ? '片語' : t === 'sentence' ? '句子' : '字';
let SRC = '';
let F = { sc: 'all', st: 'all', bd: 0, dd: 'all', q: '' }, OPEN = new Set(), RESTORE_Y = null;

/* 點進單字詳解前記住篩選、展開的字母與捲動位置，從詳解返回時還原 */
try {
  const s = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('tslState') || 'null');
  if (s) { F = Object.assign(F, s.F); OPEN = new Set(s.open || []); RESTORE_Y = s.y; sessionStorage.removeItem('tslState'); }
} catch (e) {}
document.getElementById('q').value = F.q;

function seg(id, opts, key) {
  document.getElementById(id).innerHTML = opts.map(([v, t]) =>
    `<button data-k="${key}" data-v="${v}" class="px-3 py-1.5 ${String(F[key]) === String(v) ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800'}">${t}</button>`).join('');
}

function paint() {
  const has = x => x.o || LEARNED.has(x.n);
  const bank = F.sc === 'bank';
  const BASE = bank ? ROWS.filter(has).concat(OTHERS) : ROWS;
  const haveN = ROWS.filter(has).length, gapN = ROWS.filter(gap).length;
  const per = BANDS.map(b => {
    const all = ROWS.filter(x => x.b === b), got = all.filter(has);
    return `<div class="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 p-2.5">
      <span class="inline-block text-xs rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 ${b.cls}">${b.label}</span>
      <div class="text-sm mt-1.5"><b>${got.length}</b> / ${all.length}　<span class="text-slate-400">缺 ${all.length - got.length}</span></div>
      <div class="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 mt-1.5 overflow-hidden"><div class="h-full bg-emerald-500" style="width:${Math.round(got.length / all.length * 100)}%"></div></div></div>`;
  }).join('');
  document.getElementById('sum').innerHTML = `
    <div class="text-sm mb-2">已收錄 <b>${haveN}</b> / ${ROWS.length} 字（${Math.round(haveN / ROWS.length * 100)}%），還缺 <b class="text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400">${ROWS.length - haveN}</b> 字${gapN ? `；已收錄但<a href="daily.html#tsl" class="underline">缺詳解</a> <b class="text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">${gapN}</b> 字` : ''}</div>
    <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">${per}</div>`;

  seg('fsc', [['all', '全部 TSL'], ['bank', '我的入庫']], 'sc');
  seg('fs', bank ? [['all', '全部'], ['have', '有詳解'], ['gap', '缺詳解']] : [['all', '全部'], ['have', '已收錄'], ['gap', '缺詳解'], ['miss', '還缺']], 'st');
  seg('fb', [[0, '全分數帶'], [1, '&lt;650'], [2, '650–749'], [3, '≥750']].concat(bank ? [[4, '無分數']] : []), 'bd');

  document.getElementById('fdw').innerHTML = [['all', '全部主題', bank ? BASE.length : ROWS.length, haveN], ...['1', '2', '3', '4', '5', '6', '7', 'G'].concat(bank ? ['N'] : []).map(k => { const a = BASE.filter(x => x.d === k); return [k, (k === 'G' || k === 'N' ? '' : 'D' + k + ' ') + TSL_DNAME[k], a.length, a.filter(has).length]; })]
    .map(([k, t, n, g]) => `<button data-k="dd" data-v="${k}" class="rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1.5 ${F.dd === k ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white border-indigo-600' : 'border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800'}">${t} <b>${bank ? n : g}</b>${bank ? '' : '/' + n}</button>`).join('')
    + (bank ? '' : '<button data-copy="1" class="rounded-lg border border-emerald-500 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 px-2.5 py-1.5 hover:bg-emerald-50 dark:hover:bg-emerald-950">複製目前篩選的「還缺」單字</button><button data-copy="gap" class="rounded-lg border border-amber-500 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400 px-2.5 py-1.5 hover:bg-amber-50 dark:hover:bg-amber-950">複製「缺詳解」單字</button><a href="daily.html#tsl" class="rounded-lg border border-indigo-500 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400 px-2.5 py-1.5 hover:bg-indigo-50 dark:hover:bg-indigo-950">複製 AI 提示詞 →</a>');

  const q = norm(F.q), searching = !!q;
  const pass = x => {
    const h = has(x);
    if (F.st === 'have' && (bank ? gap(x) : !h)) return false;
    if (F.st === 'miss' && h) return false;
    if (F.st === 'gap' && !gap(x)) return false;
    if (F.bd && (x.b ? x.b.k : 4) !== +F.bd) return false;
    if (F.dd !== 'all' && x.d !== F.dd) return false;
    if (q && !x.n.includes(q)) return false;
    return true;
  };
  let html = '';
  const LET = [...new Set(BASE.map(x => (x.n[0] || '#').toUpperCase()))].sort();
  LET.forEach(L => {
    const grp = BASE.filter(x => (x.n[0] || '#').toUpperCase() === L);
    const shown = grp.filter(pass);
    if (!shown.length) return;
    const got = grp.filter(has).length, pct = Math.round(got / grp.length * 100);
    const open = searching || OPEN.has(L);
    html += `<details data-l="${L}" class="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900" ${open ? 'open' : ''}>
      <summary class="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 cursor-pointer select-none">
        <span class="text-lg font-bold w-6">${L}</span>
        <span class="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400"><b class="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">${got}</b> / ${grp.length}${shown.length !== grp.length ? `　<span class="text-xs">（符合 ${shown.length}）</span>` : ''}</span>
        <span class="flex-1 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 overflow-hidden max-w-[10rem] ml-auto"><span class="block h-full bg-emerald-500" style="width:${pct}%"></span></span>
      </summary>
      <div class="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 gap-2 p-3 pt-1">${shown.map(x => {
        const hit = x.o ? { s: x.s, from: x.from } : LEARNED.get(x.n);
        const inner = `<span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${x.b ? x.b.dot : 'bg-slate-400'}"></span><span class="font-semibold truncate">${esc(x.w)}</span><span class="ml-auto text-[0.625rem] text-slate-400 shrink-0" title="主題：${TSL_DNAME[x.d]}${x.d2 ? '／次：D' + x.d2 + ' ' + TSL_DNAME[x.d2] : ''}">${x.o ? typeTag(x.type) : x.d === 'G' ? 'G' : 'D' + x.d + (x.d2 ? '·' + x.d2 : '')}</span><span class="text-xs text-slate-400 shrink-0">${x.r || '—'}</span>`;
        if (hit && hit.s === 0) return `<a href="daily.html#vocab=${encodeURIComponent(x.n)}" title="${hit.from || 'W' + hit.w + 'D' + hit.d} · 已入庫但缺詳解，點開看單字卡" class="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-amber-400 bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/30 hover:bg-amber-100 dark:hover:bg-amber-950 px-2.5 py-2 text-sm">${inner}<span class="text-[0.625rem] text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 shrink-0">缺詳解</span></a>`;
        return hit
          ? `<a href="daily.html#vocab=${encodeURIComponent(x.n)}" title="${hit.from || 'W' + hit.w + 'D' + hit.d} · 點開詳解" class="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-indigo-300 dark:border-indigo-800 hover:bg-indigo-50 dark:hover:bg-indigo-950 px-2.5 py-2 text-sm">${inner}</a>`
          : `<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 px-2.5 py-2 text-sm text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">${inner}</div>`;
      }).join('')}</div></details>`;
  });
  document.getElementById('list').innerHTML = html || '<p class="text-sm text-slate-500">沒有符合的單字。</p>';
  document.getElementById('src').textContent = SRC;
}

/* 事件 */
document.addEventListener('toggle', e => { // 記住展開的字母（搜尋時自動展開，不寫入）
  const d = e.target; if (!d.dataset || !d.dataset.l || F.q) return;
  d.open ? OPEN.add(d.dataset.l) : OPEN.delete(d.dataset.l);
}, true);
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  if (e.target.closest('a[href^="daily.html"]')) {
    try { sessionStorage.setItem('tslState', JSON.stringify({ F, open: [...OPEN], y: window.scrollY })); } catch (er) {}
    return;
  }
  const b = e.target.closest('button'); if (!b) return;
  if (b.dataset.copy) { /* 依目前篩選（狀態／分數帶／主題／搜尋）複製還缺的字，依官方詞頻排名，逗號分隔，可直接貼進 prompt */
    const qq = norm(F.q), isGap = b.dataset.copy === 'gap', t = ROWS.filter(x => (isGap ? gap(x) : !LEARNED.has(x.n)) && (!F.bd || x.b.k === +F.bd) && (F.dd === 'all' || x.d === F.dd) && (!qq || x.n.includes(qq))).sort((a, c) => a.r - c.r).map(x => x.w).join(', ');
    const ok = () => { b.textContent = '✓ 已複製'; setTimeout(paint, 1200); };
    const fb = () => { const ta = document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value = t; ta.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;opacity:0'; document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); try { document.execCommand('copy'); } catch (er) {} ta.remove(); ok(); };
    if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) navigator.clipboard.writeText(t).then(ok, fb); else fb();
    return;
  }
  if (b.dataset.k) {
    F[b.dataset.k] = b.dataset.v;
    if (F.sc !== 'bank' && (F.dd === 'N' || F.bd === '4' || F.bd === 4)) { if (F.dd === 'N') F.dd = 'all'; if (+F.bd === 4) F.bd = 0; }
    if (F.sc === 'bank' && F.st === 'miss') F.st = 'all';
    paint();
    if (F.sc === 'bank' && !OTH_LOADED) ensureOthers().then(paint);
  }
  else if (b.dataset.all) { OPEN = b.dataset.all === '1' ? new Set(LETTERS) : new Set(); paint(); }
});
document.getElementById('q').addEventListener('input', e => { F.q = e.target.value; paint(); });

/* 載入：優先讀 vocab_index.json（Scan總表與音檔.py 產生的小檔，只有已收錄的字）；沒有才退回讀整份 daily.json */
const getJ = async u => { try { const r = await fetch(u, { cache: 'no-store' }); return r.ok ? await r.json() : null; } catch (e) { return null; } };
async function boot() {
  paint();
  const ix = IX = await getJ('vocab_index.json');
  if (ix && ix.tsl) {
    LEARNED = new Map(Object.entries(ix.tsl).map(([k, v]) => [k, { w: v[0], d: v[1], s: v[2] }]));
    SRC = `已收錄資料：vocab_index.json（掃描於 ${new Date(ix.v * 1000).toLocaleString('zh-TW', { hour12: false })}）。新增單字後請重新執行 Scan總表與音檔.py 並上傳。`;
  } else {
    const j = await getJ('daily.json');
    if (j) {
      const m = new Map(), add = (word, w, d) => { const k = norm(word); if (k && !m.has(k)) m.set(k, { w, d }); };
      (Array.isArray(j) ? j : (j.items || [])).forEach(x => {
        (x.vocab || []).forEach(v => add(v.word, x.week, x.day));
        (x.extra_vocab || []).forEach(e => { if (!e.type || e.type === 'word') { add(e.text, x.week, x.day); (e.forms || []).forEach(f => add(f.w, x.week, x.day)); } });
      });
      LEARNED = m;
      SRC = '找不到 vocab_index.json，已改為即時讀取整份 daily.json（流量較大）。執行 Scan總表與音檔.py 產生 vocab_index.json 後會改讀小檔。';
    } else {
      SRC = '讀不到 vocab_index.json 與 daily.json（可能是直接雙擊開啟）。請從網站網址開啟；目前全部顯示為「還缺」。';
    }
  }
  mergeMarks();
  if (F.sc === 'bank') await ensureOthers();
  paint();
  if (RESTORE_Y != null) { window.scrollTo(0, RESTORE_Y); RESTORE_Y = null; }
}
boot();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

要新增掃描範圍就改 scan_index.py 的 MODULES 區塊並整份回傳。）
